            </main>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function() {
            try {
                const client = window.supabaseClient;
                if (!client) throw new Error("Supabase Client가 초기화되지 않았습니다.");

                // 🔥 1. 대용량 데이터 한 번에 가져오기 (Promise.all로 병렬 처리)
                // fetchDraws: 당첨번호 (가벼움)
                // fetchFeatures: 분석 데이터 (무거움, 약 5만행)
                const [drawsResult, featuresResult] = await Promise.all([
                    client.from('lotto_draws').select('*').order('round', { ascending: false }).range(0, 5000),
                    // 필요한 컬럼만 선택하여 속도 최적화
                    client.from('number_features_by_round')
                          .select('round, number, appearance_count_5, appearance_count_10, appearance_count_15, appearance_count_20')
                          .range(0, 60000) // 넉넉하게 잡음 (대략 1300회 * 45 = 58500행)
                ]);

                if (drawsResult.error) throw drawsResult.error;
                if (featuresResult.error) throw featuresResult.error;

                // 2. 당첨번호 데이터 세팅
                const data = drawsResult.data;
                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
                    document.getElementById('drawsList').innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" class="p-10 text-center">데이터가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
                    document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').style.display = 'none';
                    return;
                }
                allData = data;

                // 🔥 3. 분석 데이터 메모리 맵핑 (고속 조회를 위한 구조 변환)
                const featureData = featuresResult.data;
                featuresMap = new Map();
                
                // DB에서 가져온 배열을 Map으로 변환: { 1203: [데이터들], 1202: [데이터들] ... }
                featureData.forEach(row => {
                    if (!featuresMap.has(row.round)) {
                        featuresMap.set(row.round, []);
                    }
                    featuresMap.get(row.round).push(row);
                });

                // 초기 설정
                const latestDrawRound = allData[0].round;
                selectedRound = latestDrawRound + 1;
                
                const totalCount = allData.length;
                const displaySlider = document.getElementById('displayRange');
                displaySlider.max = totalCount;
                
                loadFilterSettings();
                populateRoundSelect();
                
                // 4. 모든 데이터 준비 완료 후 UI 렌더링 (동기식으로 실행됨 = 즉시 반영)
                // 여기서부터는 메모리에 있는 데이터를 쓰므로 매우 빠름
                calculateAndSetMinMax(currentPeriod);
                updateAnalysis();
                renderCountTable();
                updateFilterMaxValues();
                updateDisplayRange(displayLimit); 
                renderFilterZones();
                
                // 로딩 종료
                document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').style.display = 'none';
                
                // AI 분석 실행
                await refreshAIAnalysis();
                
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('데이터 로드 실패:', err);
                document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').innerHTML = `<p class="text-red-500 font-bold">데이터 로드 실패: ${err.message}</p>`;
            }
        });

        function dragStart(event, number) {
            event.dataTransfer.setData('number', number);
            event.target.classList.add('dragging');
        }

        function dragEnd(event) {
            event.target.classList.remove('dragging');
        }

        function allowDrop(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.currentTarget.classList.add('drag-over');
        }

        function dragLeave(event) {
            event.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag-over');
        }

        function dropNumber(event, type) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag-over');
            const number = parseInt(event.dataTransfer.getData('number'));
            if (type === 'exclude') {
                if (!excludeNumbers.includes(number)) {
                    if (fixedNumbers.includes(number)) { alert('이미 고정수에 포함된 번호입니다.'); return; }
                    excludeNumbers.push(number);
                    excludeNumbers.sort((a, b) => a - b);
                    renderFilterZones();
                }
            } else if (type === 'fixed') {
                if (!fixedNumbers.includes(number)) {
                    if (fixedNumbers.length >= 6) { alert('고정수는 최대 6개까지만 선택 가능합니다.'); return; }
                    if (excludeNumbers.includes(number)) { alert('이미 제외수에 포함된 번호입니다.'); return; }
                    fixedNumbers.push(number);
                    fixedNumbers.sort((a, b) => a - b);
                    renderFilterZones();
                }
            }
        }

        function removeFilterNumber(number, type) {
            if (type === 'exclude') {
                excludeNumbers = excludeNumbers.filter(n => n !== number);
            } else if (type === 'fixed') {
                fixedNumbers = fixedNumbers.filter(n => n !== number);
            }
            renderFilterZones();
        }

        function renderFilterZones() {
            const excludeZone = document.getElementById('excludeZone');
            const fixedZone = document.getElementById('fixedZone');
            const { hot, neutral, cold } = getClassifiedNumbers(currentPeriod, selectedRound);

            const getNumberClass = (n) => {
                if (hot.includes(n)) return 'hot-bg';
                if (neutral.includes(n)) return 'neutral-bg';
                if (cold.includes(n)) return 'cold-bg';
                return 'neutral-bg';
            };
            
            if (excludeNumbers.length === 0) excludeZone.innerHTML = '<p class="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center">번호를 드래그하여 추가하세요</p>';
            else excludeZone.innerHTML = excludeNumbers.map(n => `<span class="filter-number ${getNumberClass(n)}" onclick="removeFilterNumber(${n}, 'exclude')">${n}</span>`).join('');
            
            if (fixedNumbers.length === 0) fixedZone.innerHTML = '<p class="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center">번호를 드래그하여 추가하세요</p>';
            else fixedZone.innerHTML = fixedNumbers.map(n => `<span class="filter-number ${getNumberClass(n)}" onclick="removeFilterNumber(${n}, 'fixed')">${n}</span>`).join('');
        }

        function updateFilterMaxValues() {
            if (allData.length === 0) return;
            const { hot, neutral, cold } = getClassifiedNumbers(currentPeriod, selectedRound);
            document.getElementById('hotMin').max = Math.min(6, hot.length);
            document.getElementById('hotMax').max = Math.min(6, hot.length);
            document.getElementById('neutralMin').max = Math.min(6, neutral.length);
            document.getElementById('neutralMax').max = Math.min(6, neutral.length);
            document.getElementById('coldMin').max = Math.min(6, cold.length);
            document.getElementById('coldMax').max = Math.min(6, cold.length);
        }

        function populateRoundSelect() {
            const select = document.getElementById('roundSelect');
            if (allData.length === 0) return;
            const latestRound = allData[0].round;
            const nextRound = latestRound + 1;
            let options = `<option value="${nextRound}">${nextRound}회</option>`;
            allData.forEach(d => { options += `<option value="${d.round}">${d.round}회</option>`; });
            select.innerHTML = options;
            select.value = selectedRound;
        }

        // 🔥 [핵심 로직] DB 데이터 기반 분류 (동기식)
        // featuresMap에 이미 DB 데이터가 다 있으므로 꺼내 쓰기만 하면 됨
        function getClassifiedNumbers(period, fromRound) {
            // DB 데이터가 없는 경우 (미래 회차이거나 데이터 누락) -> 기존 로직으로 Fallback
            // 하지만 대부분 여기서 리턴됨
            if (featuresMap.has(fromRound)) {
                const features = featuresMap.get(fromRound);
                const hot = [], neutral = [], cold = [];
                
                features.forEach(f => {
                    let count = 0;
                    if (period === 5) count = f.appearance_count_5 || 0;
                    else if (period === 10) count = f.appearance_count_10 || 0;
                    else if (period === 15) count = f.appearance_count_15 || 0;
                    else if (period === 20) count = f.appearance_count_20 || 0;
                    
                    const criteria = CRITERIA[period];
                    
                    if (count >= criteria.hot) hot.push(f.number);
                    else if (count >= criteria.cold[1] && count <= criteria.cold[0]) cold.push(f.number);
                    else neutral.push(f.number);
                });
                return { hot, neutral, cold };
            }
            
            // 데이터가 없을 때의 안전장치 (기존 계산 로직 사용)
            return getClassifiedNumbersFallback(period, fromRound);
        }
        
        // 안전장치: DB 데이터 없을 때 직접 계산
        function getClassifiedNumbersFallback(period, fromRound) {
            const hot = [], neutral = [], cold = [];
            const latestRoundInDB = allData[0]?.round || 0;
            
            for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                let startIdx = 0;
                if (fromRound <= latestRoundInDB) {
                    const roundIdx = allData.findIndex(d => d.round === fromRound);
                    if (roundIdx === -1) { neutral.push(num); continue; }
                    startIdx = roundIdx + 1;
                }
                if (startIdx + period > allData.length) { neutral.push(num); continue; }
                const recentDraws = allData.slice(startIdx, startIdx + period);
                let count = 0;
                recentDraws.forEach(draw => { if (draw.numbers.includes(num)) count++; });
                const criteria = CRITERIA[period];
                if (count >= criteria.hot) hot.push(num);
                else if (count >= criteria.cold[1] && count <= criteria.cold[0]) cold.push(num);
                else neutral.push(num);
            }
            return { hot, neutral, cold };
        }

        function calculateAndSetMinMax(period) {
            if (allData.length === 0) return;
            let hMin = 99, hMax = -1;
            let nMin = 99, nMax = -1;
            let cMin = 99, cMax = -1;
            const analyzeLimit = period; 
            const targetData = allData.slice(0, analyzeLimit); 

            for (const draw of targetData) {
                const { hot, neutral, cold } = getClassifiedNumbers(period, draw.round);
                const hotCount = draw.numbers.filter(n => hot.includes(n)).length;
                const neutralCount = draw.numbers.filter(n => neutral.includes(n)).length;
                const coldCount = draw.numbers.filter(n => cold.includes(n)).length;

                if (hotCount < hMin) hMin = hotCount;
                if (hotCount > hMax) hMax = hotCount;
                if (neutralCount < nMin) nMin = neutralCount;
                if (neutralCount > nMax) nMax = neutralCount;
                if (coldCount < cMin) cMin = coldCount;
                if (coldCount > cMax) cMax = coldCount;
            }
            if(hMin === 99) { hMin=0; hMax=6; nMin=0; nMax=6; cMin=0; cMax=6; }
            document.getElementById('hotMin').value = hMin;
            document.getElementById('hotMax').value = hMax;
            document.getElementById('neutralMin').value = nMin;
            document.getElementById('neutralMax').value = nMax;
            document.getElementById('coldMin').value = cMin;
            document.getElementById('coldMax').value = cMax;
        }

        async function selectRound(round) {
            selectedRound = parseInt(round);
            updateAnalysis(); 
            renderCountTable(); 
            updateFilterMaxValues(); 
            updateSelectedRoundDisplay(); 
            renderFilterZones();
            await refreshAIAnalysis();
        }

        function updateSelectedRoundDisplay() {
            if (allData.length === 0) return;
            const { hot, neutral, cold } = getClassifiedNumbers(currentPeriod, selectedRound);
            const currentDraw = allData.find(d => d.round === selectedRound);
            const latestRoundInDB = allData[0].round;
            const isDraggable = selectedRound > latestRoundInDB;

            const renderNumbers = (numbers, typeClass) => {
                return numbers.sort((a, b) => a - b).map(n => {
                    const isWinning = currentDraw && currentDraw.numbers.includes(n);
                    const className = isWinning ? 'winning-bg' : typeClass;
                    const dragAttr = isDraggable ? `draggable="true" ondragstart="dragStart(event, ${n})" ondragend="dragEnd(event)"` : '';
                    const cursorClass = isDraggable ? '' : 'no-drag';
                    return `<span class="lotto-number ${className} ${cursorClass}" ${dragAttr}>${n}</span>`;
                }).join('');
            };

            document.getElementById('selectedRoundNumbers').innerHTML = `
                <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div class="space-y-2"><h4 class="text-sm font-semibold" style="color: #DC2626;">Hot (${hot.length}개)</h4><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">${renderNumbers(hot, 'hot-bg')}</div></div>
                    <div class="space-y-2"><h4 class="text-sm font-semibold" style="color: #6B7280;">Neutral (${neutral.length}개)</h4><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">${renderNumbers(neutral, 'neutral-bg')}</div></div>
                    <div class="space-y-2"><h4 class="text-sm font-semibold" style="color: #2563EB;">Cold (${cold.length}개)</h4><div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">${renderNumbers(cold, 'cold-bg')}</div></div>
                </div>`;
        }

        function renderCountTable() {
            if (allData.length === 0) return;
            const { hot, neutral, cold } = getClassifiedNumbers(currentPeriod, selectedRound);
            const countMap = { hot: {}, neutral: {}, cold: {} };
            const latestRoundInDB = allData[0].round;
            let startIdx = 0;
            if (selectedRound <= latestRoundInDB) { startIdx = allData.findIndex(d => d.round === selectedRound) + 1; }
            const recentDraws = allData.slice(startIdx, startIdx + currentPeriod);

            [hot, neutral, cold].forEach((nums, typeIdx) => {
                const type = ['hot', 'neutral', 'cold'][typeIdx];
                nums.forEach(num => {
                    let count = 0;
                    recentDraws.forEach(draw => { if (draw.numbers.includes(num)) count++; });
                    if (!countMap[type][count]) countMap[type][count] = [];
                    countMap[type][count].push(num);
                });
            });
            
            const maxCount = Math.max(...Object.keys(countMap.hot).map(Number), ...Object.keys(countMap.neutral).map(Number), ...Object.keys(countMap.cold).map(Number), 0);
            let html = '';
            for (let count = maxCount; count >= 0; count--) {
                html += `<tr class="hover:bg-gray-50"><td class="py-3 px-6 text-center font-semibold text-gray-900 border border-gray-200">${count}회</td><td class="py-3 px-8 text-center border border-gray-200">${countMap.hot[count] ? countMap.hot[count].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(', ') : '-'}</td><td class="py-3 px-6 text-center border border-gray-200">${countMap.neutral[count] ? countMap.neutral[count].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(', ') : '-'}</td><td class="py-3 px-6 text-center border border-gray-200">${countMap.cold[count] ? countMap.cold[count].sort((a, b) => a - b).join(', ') : '-'}</td></tr>`;
            }
            document.getElementById('countTableBody').innerHTML = html;
        }

        function updateFilters() {
            saveFilterSettings();
            if (filterApplied) renderDrawsList();
        }

        function toggleFilterApply() {
            saveFilterSettings();
            filterApplied = document.getElementById('filterToggle').checked;
            renderDrawsList();
        }

        function updateDisplayRange(value) {
            displayLimit = parseInt(value);
            document.getElementById('rangeLabel').textContent = value + '회';
            updateSliderBackground(value);
            renderDrawsList();
        }

        function updateSliderBackground(value) {
            const total = allData.length || 1203;
            const slider = document.getElementById('displayRange');
            if (slider) {
                const percent = ((value - 10) / (total - 10)) * 100;
                slider.style.setProperty('--value-percent', percent + '%');
            }
        }

        function toggleCellHighlight(col, val) {
            if (selectedHighlight.col === col && selectedHighlight.val === val) {
                selectedHighlight = { col: null, val: null };
            } else {
                selectedHighlight = { col: col, val: val };
            }
            renderDrawsList();
        }

        function renderDrawsList() {
            if (allData.length === 0) return;
            let filteredData = [];
            const container = document.getElementById('drawsList');
            if (!container) return;

            // 1. 필터링 (메모리 사용 - 매우 빠름)
            if (filterApplied) {
                const hotMin = parseInt(document.getElementById('hotMin').value);
                const hotMax = parseInt(document.getElementById('hotMax').value);
                const neutralMin = parseInt(document.getElementById('neutralMin').value);
                const neutralMax = parseInt(document.getElementById('neutralMax').value);
                const coldMin = parseInt(document.getElementById('coldMin').value);
                const coldMax = parseInt(document.getElementById('coldMax').value);
                
                for (const draw of allData) {
                    const { hot, neutral, cold } = getClassifiedNumbers(currentPeriod, draw.round);
                    const hotCount = draw.numbers.filter(n => hot.includes(n)).length;
                    const neutralCount = draw.numbers.filter(n => neutral.includes(n)).length;
                    const coldCount = draw.numbers.filter(n => cold.includes(n)).length;
                    
                    if (hotCount >= hotMin && hotCount <= hotMax &&
                        neutralCount >= neutralMin && neutralCount <= neutralMax &&
                        coldCount >= coldMin && coldCount <= coldMax) {
                        filteredData.push(draw);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                filteredData = [...allData];
            }
            
            container.innerHTML = '';
            const targetData = filteredData.slice(0, displayLimit);

            // 2. 렌더링 (메모리 사용 - 매우 빠름)
            targetData.forEach((draw, index) => {
                const { hot: currentHot, neutral: currentNeutral, cold: currentCold } = getClassifiedNumbers(currentPeriod, draw.round);
                
                const hotNumbers = draw.numbers.filter(n => currentHot.includes(n));
                const neutralNumbers = draw.numbers.filter(n => currentNeutral.includes(n));
                const coldNumbers = draw.numbers.filter(n => currentCold.includes(n));
                
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = 'border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer transition-colors';
                tr.onclick = () => highlightRows(index); 
                tr.id = `draw-row-${index}`;

                const numberBalls = draw.numbers.map(num => {
                    const type = currentHot.includes(num) ? 'hot' : currentNeutral.includes(num) ? 'neutral' : 'cold';
                    const className = type === 'hot' ? 'hot-bg' : type === 'neutral' ? 'neutral-bg' : 'cold-bg';
                    return `<span class="lotto-number ${className}">${num}</span>`;
                }).join('');

                const getHighlightClass = (col, val) => {
                    if (selectedHighlight.col === col && selectedHighlight.val === val) {
                        return 'bg-slate-700 text-white shadow-sm';
                    }
                    return '';
                };
                const getTextColor = (col, val, defaultColor) => {
                    if (selectedHighlight.col === col && selectedHighlight.val === val) return 'text-white';
                    return defaultColor;
                };

                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900 sticky left-0 bg-inherit border-r border-gray-200">${draw.round}회</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 sticky bg-inherit border-r border-gray-200" style="left: 80px; min-width: 280px;">
                        <div class="flex gap-1 justify-center">${numberBalls}</div>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center text-sm font-semibold border-r border-gray-200" style="color: #DC2626;">${currentHot.length}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center text-sm font-semibold border-r border-gray-200" style="color: #6B7280;">${currentNeutral.length}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center text-sm font-semibold border-r border-gray-200" style="color: #2563EB;">${currentCold.length}</td>
                    
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center text-sm border-r border-gray-200">
                        <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('hotCount', ${hotNumbers.length})" 
                              class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-full font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${getHighlightClass('hotCount', hotNumbers.length)} ${getTextColor('hotCount', hotNumbers.length, 'text-red-600')}">
                            ${hotNumbers.length}
                        </span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center text-sm border-r border-gray-200">
                        <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('neutralCount', ${neutralNumbers.length})" 
                              class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-full font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${getHighlightClass('neutralCount', neutralNumbers.length)} ${getTextColor('neutralCount', neutralNumbers.length, 'text-gray-500')}">
                            ${neutralNumbers.length}
                        </span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-3 text-center text-sm">
                        <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('coldCount', ${coldNumbers.length})" 
                              class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-full font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer ${getHighlightClass('coldCount', coldNumbers.length)} ${getTextColor('coldCount', coldNumbers.length, 'text-blue-600')}">
                            ${coldNumbers.length}
                        </span>
                    </td>
                `;
                container.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        function highlightRows(startIndex) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.row-highlight').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('row-highlight'));
            const rows = document.getElementById('drawsList').children;
            for(let i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
                if (startIndex + i < rows.length) {
                    rows[startIndex + i].classList.add('row-highlight');
                }
            }
        }

        // ==========================================================
        // [AI 분석 엔진] 핫/콜드 전용 (common_v2.js 기반)
        // ==========================================================
        async function refreshAIAnalysis() {
            const subjectData = allData[0];
            const subjectRound = subjectData.round;
            const targetRound = subjectRound + 1;
            const { hot, neutral, cold } = getClassifiedNumbers(currentPeriod, subjectRound);
            
            const targetText = `Target ${targetRound}회차_based on ${subjectRound}회차`;
            const targetEl = document.getElementById('aiTargetInfo');
            if(targetEl) targetEl.textContent = targetText;

            await window.AIAnalysis.executeAnalysis({
                containerId: 'aiAnalysisContent',
                analysisType: '핫/콜드',
                subjectRound: subjectRound,
                targetRound: targetRound,
                customData: `
# [분석 데이터]
- 기준 회차: ${subjectRound}회 (최근 ${currentPeriod}회차 빈도 기준)
- Hot(과열) 그룹: ${hot.length}개 번호 포함
- Neutral(중립) 그룹: ${neutral.length}개 번호 포함
- Cold(냉각) 그룹: ${cold.length}개 번호 포함
                `,
                customRules: `
# [5대 핵심 분석 관점]
1. 평균 회귀: 과열된 Hot 그룹의 냉각 가능성, Cold 그룹의 부활 가능성.
2. 밸런스 유지: 각 그룹별 이상적인 출현 비율(예: 2:3:1)과의 괴리 분석.
3. 에너지 흐름: 최근 Hot 그룹으로 진입한 신규 번호들의 추세.
4. 소외 구간: 장기 미출현(Cold) 번호 중 출현 임박수 선별.
5. 당첨 번호 구성 예측: 다음 회차의 Hot:Neutral:Cold 비율 예측.
                `
            });
        }

        function updateAnalysis() {
            updateSelectedRoundDisplay();
        }

        async function setPeriod(period) {
            currentPeriod = period;
            updatePeriodButtonUI(period);
            updateListCriteriaDisplay(period);
            calculateAndSetMinMax(period);
            saveFilterSettings();
            
            renderCountTable();
            updateFilterMaxValues();
            renderDrawsList();
            renderFilterZones();
            
            await refreshAIAnalysis();
        }
        
        function updatePeriodButtonUI(period) {
            [5, 10, 15, 20].forEach(p => {
                const btn = document.getElementById(`btn${p}`);
                if (p === period) {
                    btn.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors';
                } else {
                    btn.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-lg transition-colors';
                }
            });
        }
        
        function updateListCriteriaDisplay(period) {
            const label = document.getElementById('listCriteriaDisplay');
            if(label) label.textContent = `(현재 ${period}회차 기준)`;
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        var allDrawData = []; 
        var featuresMap = new Map(); // 🔥 DB 캐시 추가
        var lowHighChart = null;
        var currentRange = 100;
        var activeFilters = new Set();
        var advancedStats = {}; 
        
        var highlightTargetRound = null;
        var selectedHighlight = { col: null, val: null };

        var THEORETICAL_STATS = {
            '6:0': { count: 74613, percent: 0.92 },
            '5:1': { count: 605682, percent: 7.44 },
            '4:2': { count: 1850695, percent: 22.72 },
            '3:3': { count: 2727340, percent: 33.48 },
            '2:4': { count: 2045505, percent: 25.11 },
            '1:5': { count: 740278, percent: 9.09 },
            '0:6': { count: 100947, percent: 1.24 }
        };

        var RATIO_COLORS = {
            '6:0': { bg: '#FEE2E2', text: '#991B1B', border: '#EF4444' }, 
            '5:1': { bg: '#FFEDD5', text: '#9A3412', border: '#F97316' }, 
            '4:2': { bg: '#FEF9C3', text: '#854D0E', border: '#EAB308' }, 
            '3:3': { bg: '#DCFCE7', text: '#166534', border: '#22C55E' }, 
            '2:4': { bg: '#DBEAFE', text: '#1E40AF', border: '#3B82F6' }, 
            '1:5': { bg: '#E0E7FF', text: '#3730A3', border: '#6366F1' }, 
            '0:6': { bg: '#F3E8FF', text: '#6B21A8', border: '#A855F7' }
        };

        var RATIOS = ['6:0', '5:1', '4:2', '3:3', '2:4', '1:5', '0:6'];

        // [추가됨] 필터 설정 저장 함수
        function saveFilterSettings() {
            const toggle = document.getElementById('applyFilterCheck');
            const data = {
                selectedRatios: Array.from(activeFilters),
                enabled: toggle ? toggle.checked : true
            };
            if (window.Utils && window.Utils.saveFilter) {
                window.Utils.saveFilter('low_high_filter', data);
            }
        }

        // [추가됨] 필터 설정 불러오기 함수
        function loadFilterSettings() {
            if (window.Utils && window.Utils.loadFilter) {
                const saved = window.Utils.loadFilter('low_high_filter');
                if (saved) {
                    activeFilters = new Set(saved.selectedRatios);
                    const toggle = document.getElementById('applyFilterCheck');
                    if (toggle) toggle.checked = saved.enabled;
                    
                    updateUIState();
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        function extractNumbers(item) {
            if (Array.isArray(item.numbers) && item.numbers.length === 6) {
                return item.numbers.map(Number);
            }
            if (item.drwt_no1 !== undefined) {
                return [
                    item.drwt_no1, item.drwt_no2, item.drwt_no3,
                    item.drwt_no4, item.drwt_no5, item.drwt_no6
                ].map(Number);
            }
            if (item.no1 !== undefined) {
                return [item.no1, item.no2, item.no3, item.no4, item.no5, item.no6].map(Number);
            }
            return [];
        }

        function calculateLowHigh(numbers) {
            if (!Array.isArray(numbers) || numbers.length === 0) {
                return { low: 0, high: 0, ratio: '0:0', sum: 0, endSum: 0 };
            }
            let lowCount = 0;
            numbers.forEach(n => { if (n <= 22) lowCount++; });
            let highCount = 6 - lowCount;
            const sum = numbers.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
            const endSum = numbers.reduce((a, b) => a + (b % 10), 0);
            
            return { 
                low: lowCount, 
                high: highCount, 
                ratio: `${lowCount}:${highCount}`,
                sum: sum,
                endSum: endSum
            };
        }

        function checkCorrelation(currentDraw, allData) {
            const prevDraw = allData.find(d => d.round === currentDraw.round - 1);
            if (!prevDraw) return false;

            const prevBonus = prevDraw.bonus;
            const currentLow = currentDraw.low;
            const currentHigh = currentDraw.high;

            if (prevBonus <= 22 && currentLow >= 4) return true;
            if (prevBonus >= 23 && currentHigh >= 4) return true;

            return false;
        }

        function calculateAdvancedStats(data) {
            const stats = {};
            RATIOS.forEach(r => stats[r] = { currentStreak: 0, maxStreak: 0, currentGap: 0, totalCount: 0 });

            let gapCheck = {}; 
            RATIOS.forEach(r => gapCheck[r] = true);

            for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
                const ratio = data[i].ratio;
                for (const r of RATIOS) {
                    if (r !== ratio && gapCheck[r]) stats[r].currentGap++;
                    else if (r === ratio) gapCheck[r] = false;
                }
                if (i === 0) {
                    stats[ratio].currentStreak = 1;
                    let j = 1;
                    while (j < data.length && data[j].ratio === ratio) {
                        stats[ratio].currentStreak++;
                        j++;
                    }
                }
            }
            let tempStreak = {};
            RATIOS.forEach(r => tempStreak[r] = 0);
            const reversedData = [...data].reverse(); 
            reversedData.forEach(d => {
                const r = d.ratio;
                stats[r].totalCount++;
                tempStreak[r]++;
                RATIOS.forEach(other => { if (other !== r) tempStreak[other] = 0; });
                if (tempStreak[r] > stats[r].maxStreak) stats[r].maxStreak = tempStreak[r];
            });
            return stats;
        }

        function toggleCellHighlight(col, val) {
            if (selectedHighlight.col === col && selectedHighlight.val === val) {
                selectedHighlight = { col: null, val: null };
            } else {
                selectedHighlight = { col: col, val: val };
            }
            applyFilter(); 
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function() {
            try {
                const client = window.supabaseClient;
                if (!client) throw new Error("Supabase Client가 초기화되지 않았습니다.");

                // 🔥 DB 병렬 조회 수정 (가이드 적용)
                const [drawsResult, featuresResult] = await Promise.all([
                    client.from('lotto_draws') 
                        .select('*')
                        .order('round', { ascending: false })
                        .range(0, 5000),
                    client.from('lotto_features')
                        .select('round, low_count, high_count, total_sum, last_digit_sum')
                        .range(0, 5000)
                ]); 

                if (drawsResult.error) throw drawsResult.error;
                if (featuresResult.error) console.warn('저고 DB 로드 실패 (Fallback 사용):', featuresResult.error);

                const data = drawsResult.data;

                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
                    document.getElementById('drawsList').innerHTML = '<div class="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">데이터가 없습니다.</div>';
                    return;
                }

                // 🔥 DB 데이터를 Map으로 변환
                featuresMap = new Map();
                if (featuresResult.data && featuresResult.data.length > 0) {
                    featuresResult.data.forEach(row => {
                        featuresMap.set(row.round, {
                            low_count: row.low_count,
                            high_count: row.high_count,
                            total_sum: row.total_sum,
                            last_digit_sum: row.last_digit_sum
                        });
                    });
                    console.log(`✅ 저고 DB 데이터 로드: ${featuresResult.data.length}개`);
                }

                // 🔥 데이터 매핑 (DB 우선, Fallback 계산)
                allDrawData = data.map(item => {
                    const numbers = extractNumbers(item); 
                    const bonus = Number(item.bonus || item.bnus_no || 0);

                    // DB 데이터 우선 사용
                    const features = featuresMap.get(item.round);
                    
                    if (features) {
                        return {
                            ...item,
                            numbers,
                            bonus,
                            low: features.low_count,
                            high: features.high_count,
                            ratio: `${features.low_count}:${features.high_count}`,
                            sum: features.total_sum,
                            endSum: features.last_digit_sum
                        };
                    } else {
                        // ⚠️ Fallback: 계산 방식
                        const lh = calculateLowHigh(numbers); 
                        return { ...item, numbers, bonus, ...lh };
                    }
                });
                
                const totalCount = allDrawData.length;
                document.getElementById('rangeSlider').max = totalCount;
                document.getElementById('totalRangeDisplay').textContent = `최대 ${totalCount.toLocaleString()}회`;
                document.getElementById('preset1203').setAttribute('onclick', `setRange(${totalCount})`);
                document.getElementById('preset1203').innerText = "전체";
                
                initUI();

                // [수정됨] 저장된 설정 불러오기, 없으면 기본값(최근 10회) 사용
                if (!loadFilterSettings()) {
                    const recent10Data = allDrawData.slice(0, 10);
                    recent10Data.forEach(d => activeFilters.add(d.ratio));
                    updateUIState();
                }

                updateRange(currentRange);
                setTimeout(() => { updateSliderBackground(currentRange); }, 100);
                
                updatePredictionHeader(10);
                refreshAIAnalysis(); 

            } catch (err) { console.error('Data Load Error:', err); }
        });
        
        function initUI() {
            const statContainer = document.getElementById('statCardsContainer');
            statContainer.innerHTML = '';
            RATIOS.forEach(ratio => {
                const color = RATIO_COLORS[ratio];
                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.className = `stat-card group transition-all`;
                div.id = `card-${ratio.replace(':', '_')}`;
                div.onclick = () => toggleFilter(ratio);
                div.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="ratio-badge mb-2" style="background:${color.bg}; color:${color.text}; border:1px solid ${color.border}">
                        ${ratio}
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-end gap-1">
                        <span class="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 ratio-val" id="val-${ratio.replace(':', '_')}">-</span>
                        <span class="text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium mb-1">%</span>
                    </div>
                `;
                statContainer.appendChild(div);
            });

            const filterContainer = document.getElementById('ratioFilters');
            filterContainer.innerHTML = '';
            RATIOS.forEach(ratio => {
                const btn = document.createElement('button');
                btn.className = 'filter-btn shadow-sm text-sm';
                btn.id = `btn-${ratio.replace(':', '_')}`;
                btn.textContent = ratio;
                btn.onclick = () => toggleFilter(ratio);
                filterContainer.appendChild(btn);
            });
            updateUIState();
            initChart();
        }

        function toggleFilter(ratio) {
            if (activeFilters.has(ratio)) activeFilters.delete(ratio);
            else activeFilters.add(ratio);
            updateUIState();
            applyFilter();
        }

        function updateUIState() {
            RATIOS.forEach(ratio => {
                const safeId = ratio.replace(':', '_');
                const card = document.getElementById(`card-${safeId}`);
                const btn = document.getElementById(`btn-${safeId}`);
                const isActive = activeFilters.has(ratio);
                if (isActive) {
                    card.classList.add('active'); card.classList.remove('hover:bg-gray-50');
                    btn.classList.add('active');
                } else {
                    card.classList.remove('active'); card.classList.add('hover:bg-gray-50');
                    btn.classList.remove('active');
                }
            });
        }
        
        function updateRange(value) {
            currentRange = parseInt(value);
            document.getElementById('rangeDisplay').textContent = value + '회';
            updateSliderBackground(value);
            updateStatistics();
            updatePresetButtons(currentRange);
            applyFilter();
            refreshAIAnalysis();
        }
        
        function updateStatistics() {
            if (!allDrawData.length) return;
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            const total = data.length;
            
            advancedStats = calculateAdvancedStats(allDrawData);
            const counts = {};
            RATIOS.forEach(r => counts[r] = 0);
            data.forEach(d => { if (counts[d.ratio] !== undefined) counts[d.ratio]++; });

            RATIOS.forEach(ratio => {
                const safeId = ratio.replace(':', '_');
                const count = counts[ratio] || 0;
                const percent = total > 0 ? ((count / total) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0;
                document.getElementById(`val-${safeId}`).textContent = percent;
            });

            const advContainer = document.getElementById('advancedStatsContainer');
            advContainer.innerHTML = '';
            RATIOS.forEach(ratio => {
                const s = advancedStats[ratio];
                const color = RATIO_COLORS[ratio];
                const isUrgent = s.currentGap > 10;
                const urgentClass = isUrgent ? 'border-2 border-red-400 bg-red-50' : 'border border-gray-200 bg-white';

                advContainer.innerHTML += `
                    <div class="p-3 rounded-lg ${urgentClass} shadow-sm text-center">
                        <div class="text-xs font-bold mb-1" style="color:${color.text}">${ratio}</div>
                        <div class="text-[10px] text-gray-500">현재연속 / 최대</div>
                        <div class="font-bold text-gray-800">${s.currentStreak}회 / ${s.maxStreak}회</div>
                        <div class="mt-2 text-[10px] text-gray-500">미출현(Gap)</div>
                        <div class="font-bold ${isUrgent ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-blue-600'}">${s.currentGap}회 전</div>
                    </div>
                `;
            });
            updateChart(counts, total);
        }

        function applyFilter() {
            saveFilterSettings(); // [추가됨] 변경 시 자동 저장
            let data = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            renderDrawsList(data);
        }

        function renderDrawsList(data) {
            data.sort((a, b) => b.round - a.round);
            const container = document.getElementById('drawsList');
            container.innerHTML = '';
            
            if (data.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">데이터가 없습니다.</div>';
                return;
            }
            
            data.forEach(draw => {
                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.onclick = () => highlightRecent10(draw.round);
                // 🔹 w-full 적용하여 가로 채움
                div.className = 'flex items-center gap-1 py-3 px-6 border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors cursor-pointer w-full text-center';

                if (highlightTargetRound && draw.round <= highlightTargetRound && draw.round > highlightTargetRound - 10) {
                    div.classList.add('row-highlight');
                }
                
                const numbersHtml = draw.numbers.map(num => {
                    const colorClass = (num <= 22) ? 'ball-low' : 'ball-high';
                    return `<span class="ball ${colorClass}">${String(num).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`
                }).join('');
                
                const bonusColor = (draw.bonus <= 22) ? 'ball-low' : 'ball-high';
                const bonusHtml = `<span class="ball ${bonusColor}">${String(draw.bonus).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                const isCorrelated = checkCorrelation(draw, allDrawData);

                const badgeColor = RATIO_COLORS[draw.ratio];
                const count10 = countInRecent(draw.round, draw.ratio, 10);
                const count15 = countInRecent(draw.round, draw.ratio, 15);
                const count20 = countInRecent(draw.round, draw.ratio, 20);

                const getHighlightClass = (col, val) => {
                    if (selectedHighlight.col === col && selectedHighlight.val === val) {
                        return 'bg-[#334155] text-white rounded-full shadow-sm';
                    }
                    return '';
                };
                
                const getTextColor = (col, val, defaultColor) => {
                    if (selectedHighlight.col === col && selectedHighlight.val === val) {
                        return 'text-white';
                    }
                    return defaultColor;
                };

                // 🔹 리스트 행 구조 수정 (당첨번호 270px로 확대)
                div.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-600 w-14 shrink-0 text-center">${draw.round}</span>
                    
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                         <div class="flex items-center w-[270px] justify-center"> ${numbersHtml}
                        </div>
                        <div class="w-12 flex justify-center shrink-0">
                            ${bonusHtml}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('ratio', '${draw.ratio}')"
                          class="flex-1 flex justify-center cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-full py-1 transition-colors ${getHighlightClass('ratio', draw.ratio)}">
                        <span class="ratio-badge ${getTextColor('ratio', draw.ratio, '')}" style="${(selectedHighlight.col === 'ratio' && selectedHighlight.val === draw.ratio) ? '' : `background:${badgeColor.bg}; color:${badgeColor.text}; border:1px solid ${badgeColor.border}`}">
                            ${draw.ratio}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    <div class="flex-1 flex justify-center text-center">
                        ${isCorrelated ? '<span class="correlation-badge">상관</span>' : '<span class="text-gray-300">-</span>'}
                    </div>

                    <div class="flex-1 flex justify-center">
                        <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('sum', ${draw.sum})"
                              class="text-sm w-16 shrink-0 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-full transition-colors flex items-center justify-center ${getHighlightClass('sum', draw.sum)} ${getTextColor('sum', draw.sum, 'text-gray-700')}">
                            ${draw.sum}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="flex-1 flex justify-center">
                        <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('endSum', ${draw.endSum})"
                              class="text-sm w-16 shrink-0 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-full transition-colors flex items-center justify-center ${getHighlightClass('endSum', draw.endSum)} ${getTextColor('endSum', draw.endSum, 'text-gray-700')}">
                            ${draw.endSum}
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    <div class="flex-1 flex justify-center">
                        <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('10', ${count10})"
                              class="text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-full transition-colors flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 ${getHighlightClass('10', count10)} ${getTextColor('10', count10, 'text-gray-800')}">
                            ${count10}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="flex-1 flex justify-center">
                         <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('15', ${count15})"
                              class="text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-full transition-colors flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 ${getHighlightClass('15', count15)} ${getTextColor('15', count15, 'text-gray-800')}">
                            ${count15}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                   
                    <div class="flex-1 flex justify-center">
                         <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('20', ${count20})"
                              class="text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-full transition-colors flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 ${getHighlightClass('20', count20)} ${getTextColor('20', count20, 'text-gray-800')}">
                            ${count20}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                `;
                container.appendChild(div);
            });
        }

        function countInRecent(targetRound, targetRatio, range) {
            const index = allDrawData.findIndex(d => d.round === targetRound);
            if (index === -1) return '-';
            const slice = allDrawData.slice(index, index + range);
            return slice.filter(d => d.ratio === targetRatio).length;
        }

        function highlightRecent10(round) {
            highlightTargetRound = round;
            applyFilter();
        }

        function updatePredictionHeader(range) {
            if (allDrawData.length === 0) return;
            const recentData = allDrawData.slice(0, range - 1);
            const counts = {};
            RATIOS.forEach(r => counts[r] = 0);
            recentData.forEach(d => { if (counts[d.ratio] !== undefined) counts[d.ratio]++; });
            const parts = RATIOS.map(r => {
                const count = counts[r] || 0;
                return `${r} ${(count + 1)}개`; 
            });
            const summaryDiv = document.getElementById('predictionSummary');
            summaryDiv.innerHTML = `<strong>${range}회차 예상 :</strong> ${parts.join(' / ')}`;
        }
        
        function initChart() {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('lowHighChart').getContext('2d');
            lowHighChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels: RATIOS,
                    datasets: [{
                        label: '비율(%)',
                        data: Array(7).fill(0),
                        backgroundColor: RATIOS.map(r => RATIO_COLORS[r].border), 
                        borderWidth: 0,
                        borderRadius: 4,
                        barPercentage: 0.6
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { display: false },
                        datalabels: {
                            anchor: 'end', align: 'top', color: '#374151', font: { weight: 'bold', size: 11 },
                            formatter: (val) => val > 0 ? val + '%' : ''
                        }
                    },
                    scales: {
                        y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { display: false }, max: 100 },
                        x: { grid: { display: false } }
                    },
                    onClick: (e, elements) => {
                        if (elements.length > 0) {
                            const index = elements[0].index;
                            toggleFilter(RATIOS[index]);
                        }
                    }
                },
                plugins: [ChartDataLabels]
            });
        }
        
        function updateChart(counts, total) {
            if (!lowHighChart) return;
            const values = RATIOS.map(r => total > 0 ? ((counts[r] || 0) / total * 100).toFixed(1) : 0);
            const bgColors = RATIOS.map(r => {
                const baseColor = RATIO_COLORS[r].border;
                if (activeFilters.size === 0 || activeFilters.has(r)) return baseColor;
                return baseColor + '40';
            });
            lowHighChart.data.datasets[0].data = values;
            lowHighChart.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor = bgColors;
            lowHighChart.update();
        }

        function openStatsModal() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('modalStatsBody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            const total = data.length;
            const counts = {};
            RATIOS.forEach(r => counts[r] = 0);
            data.forEach(d => { if(counts[d.ratio] !== undefined) counts[d.ratio]++; });

            RATIOS.forEach(ratio => {
                const count = counts[ratio];
                const percent = total > 0 ? ((count/total)*100).toFixed(1) : 0;
                const isSelected = activeFilters.has(ratio);
                const theo = THEORETICAL_STATS[ratio];
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = 'hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors';
                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center"><input type="checkbox" class="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600 rounded focus:ring-blue-500" ${isSelected ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleFilter('${ratio}'); openStatsModal();" /></td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 font-bold" style="color:${RATIO_COLORS[ratio].text}">${ratio}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center text-gray-600">${theo.count.toLocaleString()}</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center text-gray-600">${theo.percent}%</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center font-bold text-gray-900">${count}회</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center font-bold text-blue-600">${percent}%</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-4"><div class="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2.5"><div class="h-2.5 rounded-full" style="width: ${percent}%; background-color:${RATIO_COLORS[ratio].border}"></div></div></td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(tr);
            });
            document.getElementById('statsModal').classList.remove('hidden');
        }
        function closeStatsModal() { document.getElementById('statsModal').classList.add('hidden'); }

        function updateSliderBackground(value) {
            const total = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const slider = document.getElementById('rangeSlider');
            const percent = ((value - 1) / (total - 1)) * 100;
            slider.style.setProperty('--value-percent', percent + '%');
        }
        function setRange(value) {
            const maxVal = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const safeValue = value > maxVal ? maxVal : value;
            document.getElementById('rangeSlider').value = safeValue;
            updateRange(safeValue);
        }
        function updatePresetButtons(value) {
            const maxVal = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const ids = {50: 'preset50', 100: 'preset100', 500: 'preset500', 1203: 'preset1203'};
            Object.keys(ids).forEach(key => {
                const btn = document.getElementById(ids[key]);
                const target = key == 1203 ? maxVal : parseInt(key);
                if (target == value) btn.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors';
                else btn.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 rounded-lg transition-colors';
            });
        }

        async function refreshAIAnalysis() {
            const subjectData = allDrawData[0];
            const subjectRound = subjectData ? subjectData.round : 0;
            const targetRound = subjectRound + 1;
            
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            const recentDraws = data.slice(0, 5).map(d => `[${d.round}회: ${d.ratio}]`).join(', ');
            
            const targetEl = document.getElementById('aiTargetInfo');
            if(targetEl) targetEl.textContent = `Target ${targetRound}회차_based on ${subjectRound}회차`;

            await window.AIAnalysis.executeAnalysis({
                containerId: 'aiAnalysisContent', 
                analysisType: '저고 비율',            
                subjectRound: subjectRound,
                targetRound: targetRound,
                customData: `
# [저고 비율 심층 분석 데이터]
- 최근 5회차 저:고 비율 흐름: ${recentDraws}
- 이상적인 균형 비율: 3:3 또는 4:2, 2:4
- 특징: 저번호(1~22), 고번호(23~45) 간의 쏠림 현상 및 회귀 본능 분석 필요.
                `,
                customRules: `
# [분석 요구사항]
1. 추세 분석 (Trend): 최근 저번호 혹은 고번호 쪽으로 쏠림 현상이 있는지 분석하라.
2. 패턴 (Pattern): 극단적 비율(6:0, 0:6, 5:1, 1:5) 출현 후 회귀 패턴을 분석하라.
3. 추천 (Recommendation): 다음 회차에 가장 유력한 저:고 비율(예: 3:3)을 제시하라.
                `
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        // [추가됨] 필터 설정 저장 함수
        function saveFilterSettings() {
            const data = {
                useRangeFilter: document.getElementById('rangeFilterToggle').checked,
                useCustomFilter: document.getElementById('customFilterToggle').checked,
                ranges: {
                    r1Min: document.getElementById('range1Min').value,
                    r1Max: document.getElementById('range1Max').value,
                    r2Min: document.getElementById('range2Min').value,
                    r2Max: document.getElementById('range2Max').value,
                    r3Min: document.getElementById('range3Min').value,
                    r3Max: document.getElementById('range3Max').value,
                    r4Min: document.getElementById('range4Min').value,
                    r4Max: document.getElementById('range4Max').value
                }
            };
            if(window.Utils && window.Utils.saveFilter) {
                window.Utils.saveFilter('missing_filter', data);
            }
        }

        // [추가됨] 필터 설정 불러오기 함수
        function loadFilterSettings() {
            if(window.Utils && window.Utils.loadFilter) {
                const data = window.Utils.loadFilter('missing_filter');
                if (data) {
                    // 토글 상태 복구
                    const rangeToggle = document.getElementById('rangeFilterToggle');
                    if(rangeToggle) {
                        rangeToggle.checked = data.useRangeFilter;
                        // 전역 변수 동기화 (하단 스크립트 로드 후 실행됨)
                        if(typeof useRangeFilter !== 'undefined') useRangeFilter = data.useRangeFilter;
                    }
                    
                    const customToggle = document.getElementById('customFilterToggle');
                    if(customToggle) {
                        customToggle.checked = data.useCustomFilter;
                        if(typeof useCustomFilter !== 'undefined') useCustomFilter = data.useCustomFilter;
                    }

                    // 범위 값 복구
                    if (data.ranges) {
                        if(document.getElementById('range1Min')) document.getElementById('range1Min').value = data.ranges.r1Min;
                        if(document.getElementById('range1Max')) document.getElementById('range1Max').value = data.ranges.r1Max;
                        if(document.getElementById('range2Min')) document.getElementById('range2Min').value = data.ranges.r2Min;
                        if(document.getElementById('range2Max')) document.getElementById('range2Max').value = data.ranges.r2Max;
                        if(document.getElementById('range3Min')) document.getElementById('range3Min').value = data.ranges.r3Min;
                        if(document.getElementById('range3Max')) document.getElementById('range3Max').value = data.ranges.r3Max;
                        if(document.getElementById('range4Min')) document.getElementById('range4Min').value = data.ranges.r4Min;
                        if(document.getElementById('range4Max')) document.getElementById('range4Max').value = data.ranges.r4Max;
                    }
                    return true; // 로드 성공
                }
            }
            return false; // 저장된 데이터 없음
        }
    </script>

        }

        /* 마커 스타일 */

            width: 36px; height: 36px; border-radius: 50%; display: inline-flex;

            color: white; margin: 2px; cursor: grab; user-select: none;
        }
        .lotto-number:active { cursor: grabbing; }
        .lotto-number.dragging { opacity: 0.5; }
