    <script>
        // 전역 상태 변수
        let allData = [];
        let featuresMap = new Map(); // 🔥 DB 캐시 추가
        let useRangeFilter = true;
        let useCustomFilter = false;
        
        let displayLimit = 100;
        let selectedRound = 1204;
        let currentMiss = {};
        let missHistory = {};
        let hitCounts = new Array(46).fill(0);
        let customFilters = [];
        let filterIdCounter = 1;
        let maxMissCount = 0;
        let selectedStatGroup = null; // 선택된 통계 그룹 (1, 2, 3, 4)
        
        // 🔥 추가된 상태: 셀 하이라이트 (컬럼 타입, 값)
        let selectedCellHighlight = { type: null, val: null };

        async function loadData() {
            try {
                if (!window.supabaseClient) {
                    if (typeof supabase !== 'undefined') {
                        const URL = 'https://dkcflmyoscudawleglzb.supabase.co';
                        const KEY = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9.eyJpc3MiOiJzdXBhYmFzZSIsInJlZiI6ImRrY2ZsbXlvc2N1ZGF3bGVnbHpiIiwicm9sZSI6ImFub24iLCJpYXQiOjE3Njc1NDQ4OTAsImV4cCI6MjA4MzEyMDg5MH0.haAvbpScvMJv1uqH_tk-0fUlJNCpHnlWBtYzX6YFweo';
                        window.supabaseClient = supabase.createClient(URL, KEY);
                    } else {
                        throw new Error('Supabase SDK 스크립트가 로드되지 않았습니다.');
                    }
                }

                const client = window.supabaseClient;

                // 🔥 DB 병렬 조회: 당첨번호 + 미출현 데이터(DB)
                const [drawsResult, featuresResult] = await Promise.all([
                    client.from('lotto_draws')
                          .select('*')
                          .order('round', { ascending: false })
                          .range(0, 5000),
                    client.from('number_features_by_round')
                          .select('round, number, missing_count, last_appearance_round')
                          .range(0, 60000)  // 충분한 범위 설정
                ]);

                if (drawsResult.error) throw drawsResult.error;
                if (featuresResult.error) console.warn('미출현 DB 로드 실패 (Fallback 사용):', featuresResult.error);
                
                const data = drawsResult.data;
                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
                    document.getElementById('drawsList').innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="10" class="p-10 text-center">데이터가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
                    return;
                }

                // 🔥 DB 데이터를 Map으로 변환
                featuresMap = new Map();
                if (featuresResult.data && featuresResult.data.length > 0) {
                    featuresResult.data.forEach(row => {
                        if (!featuresMap.has(row.round)) {
                            featuresMap.set(row.round, {});
                        }
                        featuresMap.get(row.round)[row.number] = {
                            missing_count: row.missing_count,
                            last_appearance_round: row.last_appearance_round
                        };
                    });
                    console.log(`✅ 미출현 DB 데이터 로드: ${featuresResult.data.length}개`);
                }

                allData = data;
                if (allData.length > 0) {
                    const latestRound = allData[0].round;
                    selectedRound = latestRound + 1;
                }

                calculateMissData();
                calculateHitCounts();
                populateRoundSelect();
                renderMissStatsTable();
                
                updateAnalysis();
                refreshAIAnalysis();

                renderMissGroupTable();
                
                // [수정됨] 저장된 필터 로드 시도, 없으면 기본값(최근 10회 기준) 적용
                if (!loadFilterSettings()) {
                    applyRecent10Stats();
                }
                
                updateFilterMaxValues();
                renderDrawsList();
                
                const displaySlider = document.getElementById('displayRange');
                displaySlider.max = allData.length;
                updateDisplayRange(displayLimit);

                selectRound(selectedRound);
                
                const savedLatestRound = localStorage.getItem('latestLottoRound');
                if (allData.length > 0) {
                    const currentLatest = allData[0].round;
                    if (savedLatestRound && parseInt(savedLatestRound) < currentLatest) {
                        localStorage.removeItem('missGroupFilters');
                        localStorage.removeItem('missGroupFilterCounter');
                        customFilters = [];
                        filterIdCounter = 1;
                    } else {
                        loadSavedFilters();
                    }
                    localStorage.setItem('latestLottoRound', currentLatest.toString());
                }
                
                renderFiltersList();
                
                // [수정됨] 저장된 필터 로드로 인해 불필요할 수 있으나 안전장치로 유지
                document.getElementById('customFilterToggle').checked = useCustomFilter;

            } catch (err) {
                console.error('데이터 로드 실패:', err);
                document.getElementById('drawsList').innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="10" class="p-10 text-center text-red-500">데이터 로드 실패<br>${err.message}</td></tr>`;
            }
        }

        function updateFilters() {
            saveFilterSettings(); // [추가됨] 변경사항 저장
            if (useRangeFilter) renderDrawsList();
        }

        function toggleRangeFilter() {
            const toggle = document.getElementById('rangeFilterToggle');
            useRangeFilter = toggle.checked;
            saveFilterSettings(); // [추가됨] 변경사항 저장
            renderDrawsList();
        }

        function toggleCustomFilter() {
            const toggle = document.getElementById('customFilterToggle');
            useCustomFilter = toggle.checked;
            saveFilterSettings(); // [추가됨] 변경사항 저장
            renderDrawsList();
        }

        // --- 통계 카드 하이라이트 기능 ---
        function highlightStatCard(range) {
            // 이미 선택된 것을 다시 누르면 해제
            if (selectedStatGroup === range) {
                selectedStatGroup = null;
            } else {
                selectedStatGroup = range;
                // 선택 시 해당 그룹 번호들을 필터에 추가
                addGroupToFilter(range);
            }
            updateStatCardStyles();
        }

        function updateStatCardStyles() {
            const ranges = [
                { id: 1, color: '#E64A19' },
                { id: 2, color: '#4B5563' },
                { id: 3, color: '#16A34A' },
                { id: 4, color: '#1A6DFF' }
            ];

            ranges.forEach(item => {
                const card = document.getElementById(`statCard${item.id}`);
                const countSpan = document.getElementById(`cardCount${item.id}`);
                const titleSpan = card.querySelector('span:first-child');

                if (selectedStatGroup === item.id) {
                    // 활성화 스타일
                    card.classList.add('active');
                    card.style.backgroundColor = item.color;
                    countSpan.style.color = 'white';
                    titleSpan.classList.remove('text-gray-500');
                    titleSpan.classList.add('text-white', 'opacity-90');
                } else {
                    // 비활성화(기본) 스타일 복구
                    card.classList.remove('active');
                    card.style.backgroundColor = 'white';
                    countSpan.style.color = item.color;
                    titleSpan.classList.remove('text-white', 'opacity-90');
                    titleSpan.classList.add('text-gray-500');
                }
            });
        }

        // ... 기존 함수들 ...
        
        function applyRecent10Stats() {
            if (allData.length < 10) return;
            const recent10 = allData.slice(0, 10);
            const stats = { 1: { min: 6, max: 0 }, 2: { min: 6, max: 0 }, 3: { min: 6, max: 0 }, 4: { min: 6, max: 0 } };

            recent10.forEach(draw => {
                const miss = missHistory[draw.round];
                if (!miss) return;
                const counts = { 1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0 };
                draw.numbers.forEach(num => {
                    const range = getMissRange(miss[num]);
                    counts[range]++;
                });
                for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
                    stats[i].min = Math.min(stats[i].min, counts[i]);
                    stats[i].max = Math.max(stats[i].max, counts[i]);
                }
            });

            for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
                document.getElementById(`range${i}Min`).value = stats[i].min;
                document.getElementById(`range${i}Max`).value = stats[i].max;
            }
        }

        function calculateHitCounts() {
            allData.forEach(draw => {
                draw.numbers.forEach(num => {
                    hitCounts[num]++;
                });
            });
        }

        function calculateMissData() {
            // 🔥 DB 데이터 우선 사용 (최적화)
            if (featuresMap.size > 0) {
                missHistory = {};
                maxMissCount = 0;
                
                // DB에서 missHistory 생성 (매우 빠름)
                for (const [round, features] of featuresMap) {
                    missHistory[round] = {};
                    for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                        const miss = features[num]?.missing_count || 1;
                        missHistory[round][num] = miss;
                        maxMissCount = Math.max(maxMissCount, miss);
                    }
                }
                
                // currentMiss 설정 (가장 최신 회차 데이터)
                if (allData.length > 0) {
                    const latestRound = allData[0].round + 1;
                    // 최신 데이터가 있다면 매핑, 없다면 빈 객체
                    currentMiss = missHistory[latestRound] || {};
                }
                
                console.log(`✅ 미출현 히스토리 생성 완료 (DB 기반)`);
                return;
            }
            
            // ⚠️ Fallback: 기존 계산 방식 (DB 데이터 없을 시 안전장치)
            console.log(`⚠️ DB 데이터 없음 - Fallback 계산 사용`);
            const missCount = {};
            for (let i = 1; i <= 45; i++) { missCount[i] = 1; }
            
            if (!allData || allData.length === 0) return;
            const sortedData = [...allData].sort((a, b) => a.round - b.round);
            if (sortedData.length === 0) return;
            const latestRoundInDB = sortedData[sortedData.length - 1].round;
            
            sortedData.forEach(draw => {
                missHistory[draw.round] = { ...missCount };
                for (let i = 1; i <= 45; i++) {
                    if (draw.numbers.includes(i)) { missCount[i] = 1; } 
                    else { missCount[i]++; }
                }
            });
            missHistory[latestRoundInDB + 1] = { ...missCount };
            currentMiss = { ...missCount };
            maxMissCount = Math.max(...Object.values(missCount));
        }

        function renderMissStatsTable() {
            const displayRounds = Math.max(maxMissCount, 20);
            const displayData = allData.slice(0, displayRounds);
            const nextRound = allData[0].round + 1;
            const latestMiss = missHistory[nextRound] || currentMiss;
            
            document.getElementById('maxMissRounds').textContent = displayRounds;
            
            const numberOrder = Array.from({ length: 45 }, (e, i) => i + 1);
            numberOrder.sort((a, b) => latestMiss[a] - latestMiss[b]);
            
            const recentHits = { 5: {}, 10: {}, 15: {}, 20: {} };
            for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                recentHits[5][num] = 0; recentHits[10][num] = 0; recentHits[15][num] = 0; recentHits[20][num] = 0;
            }
            
            allData.slice(0, 20).forEach((draw, idx) => {
                draw.numbers.forEach(num => {
                    if (idx < 5) recentHits[5][num]++;
                    if (idx < 10) recentHits[10][num]++;
                    if (idx < 15) recentHits[15][num]++;
                    if (idx < 20) recentHits[20][num]++;
                });
            });
            
            const summaryStyle = 'background-color: #FFFBEB;';
            const thead = document.getElementById('missStatsHeader');
            let headerHTML = '<th class="round-col">회차</th>';
            numberOrder.forEach(num => { headerHTML += `<th class="number-header">${num}</th>`; });
            thead.innerHTML = headerHTML;
            
            const tbody = document.getElementById('missStatsBody');
            let bodyHTML = '';
            
            bodyHTML += `<tr><td class="round-col font-bold" style="${summaryStyle}">H/N/C</td>`;
            numberOrder.forEach((num) => {
                const count = recentHits[10][num];
                let label, color;
                if (count >= 3) { label = 'H'; color = '#EF4444'; } 
                else if (count >= 1) { label = 'N'; color = '#6B7280'; } 
                else { label = 'C'; color = '#3B82F6'; }
                bodyHTML += `<td class="font-bold" style="${summaryStyle} color: ${color};">${label}</td>`;
            });
            bodyHTML += '</tr>';
            
            [5, 10, 15, 20].forEach(range => {
                bodyHTML += `<tr><td class="round-col font-semibold" style="${summaryStyle}">최근${range}</td>`;
                numberOrder.forEach((num) => {
                    const count = recentHits[range][num];
                    let color = '#9CA3AF';
                    if (range===5 && count>=2) color='#DC2626'; else if(range===5 && count===1) color='#F59E0B';
                    if (range===10 && count>=3) color='#DC2626'; else if(range===10 && count>=1) color='#F59E0B';
                    if (range===15 && count>=4) color='#DC2626'; else if(range===15 && count>=2) color='#F59E0B';
                    if (range===20 && count>=5) color='#DC2626'; else if(range===20 && count>=3) color='#F59E0B';
                    
                    bodyHTML += `<td class="font-semibold" style="${summaryStyle} color: ${color};">${count}</td>`;
                });
                bodyHTML += '</tr>';
            });
            
            displayData.forEach((draw, index) => {
                const prevDraw = allData[index + 1];
                const prevNumbers = prevDraw ? prevDraw.numbers : [];
                
                bodyHTML += `<tr><td class="round-col">${draw.round}회</td>`;
                numberOrder.forEach(num => {
                    if (draw.numbers.includes(num)) {
                        if (prevNumbers.includes(num)) {
                            const missCount = missHistory[draw.round][num];
                            const carryClass = getCarryClass(missCount);
                            bodyHTML += `<td><div class="flex justify-center items-center h-full"><span class="marker ${carryClass}"></span></div></td>`;
                        } else {
                            bodyHTML += `<td><div class="flex justify-center items-center h-full"><span class="marker marker-hit"></span></div></td>`;
                        }
                    } else {
                        const missCount = missHistory[draw.round][num];
                        const missClass = missCount >= 10 ? 'miss-high' : 'cell-miss';
                        bodyHTML += `<td><span class="${missClass}">${missCount}</span></td>`;
                    }
                });
                bodyHTML += '</tr>';
            });
            tbody.innerHTML = bodyHTML;
        }

        function getMissClass(missCount) {
            if (missCount <= 5) return 'miss-0-5';
            if (missCount <= 10) return 'miss-6-10';
            if (missCount <= 15) return 'miss-11-15';
            return 'miss-16-plus';
        }
        function getCarryClass(missCount) {
            if (missCount <= 5) return 'marker-carry-0-5';
            if (missCount <= 10) return 'marker-carry-6-10';
            if (missCount <= 15) return 'marker-carry-11-15';
            return 'marker-carry-16-plus';
        }
        function getMissRange(missCount) {
            if (missCount <= 5) return 1;
            if (missCount <= 10) return 2;
            if (missCount <= 15) return 3;
            return 4;
        }

        function populateRoundSelect() {
            const select = document.getElementById('roundSelect');
            if (allData.length === 0) return;
            const latestRound = allData[0].round;
            const nextRound = latestRound + 1;
            let options = `<option value="${nextRound}">${nextRound}회</option>`;
            options += allData.map(d => `<option value="${d.round}">${d.round}회</option>`).join('');
            select.innerHTML = options;
            select.value = selectedRound;
        }

        function selectRound(round) {
            selectedRound = parseInt(round);
            updateSelectedRoundDisplay();
        }

        function dragStart(event, number) {
            event.dataTransfer.setData('number', number);
            event.target.classList.add('dragging');
        }
        function dragEnd(event) { event.target.classList.remove('dragging'); }
        function allowDrop(event) { event.preventDefault(); event.currentTarget.classList.add('drag-over'); }
        function dragLeave(event) { event.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag-over'); }
        
        function dropNumber(event, filterId) {
            event.preventDefault();
            event.currentTarget.classList.remove('drag-over');
            const number = parseInt(event.dataTransfer.getData('number'));
            const filter = customFilters.find(f => f.id === filterId);
            
            if (filter) {
                const isUsedInOtherFilter = customFilters.some(f => f.id !== filterId && f.numbers.includes(number));
                if (isUsedInOtherFilter) return;
                
                const uniqueNumbers = new Set(filter.numbers);
                uniqueNumbers.add(number);
                filter.numbers = Array.from(uniqueNumbers).sort((a, b) => a - b);
                if (filter.maxCount > 6) filter.maxCount = 6;
                renderFiltersList();
            }
        }

        function removeNumberFromFilter(filterId, number) {
            const filter = customFilters.find(f => f.id === filterId);
            if (filter) {
                filter.numbers = filter.numbers.filter(n => n !== number);
                renderFiltersList();
            }
        }

        function addNewFilter() {
            customFilters.push({
                id: filterIdCounter++,
                name: `미출현그룹${customFilters.length + 1}`,
                numbers: [],
                enabled: false,
                minCount: 0,
                maxCount: 0
            });
            renderFiltersList();
        }

        function updateFilterMinMax(filterId, type, value) {
            const filter = customFilters.find(f => f.id === filterId);
            if (filter) {
                const numValue = parseInt(value) || 0;
                const maxLimit = 6; 
                if (type === 'min') filter.minCount = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxLimit, numValue));
                else filter.maxCount = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxLimit, numValue));
            }
        }

        function toggleFilter(filterId) {
            const filter = customFilters.find(f => f.id === filterId);
            if (filter) filter.enabled = !filter.enabled;
        }

        function updateFilterName(filterId, newName) {
            const filter = customFilters.find(f => f.id === filterId);
            if (filter) filter.name = newName;
        }

        function deleteFilter(filterId) {
            customFilters = customFilters.filter(f => f.id !== filterId);
            renderFiltersList();
        }

        function saveFilters() {
            customFilters.forEach(filter => {
                filter.numbers = [...new Set(filter.numbers)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
            });
            localStorage.setItem('missGroupFilters', JSON.stringify(customFilters));
            localStorage.setItem('missGroupFilterCounter', filterIdCounter.toString());
        }

        function loadSavedFilters() {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem('missGroupFilters');
            const savedCounter = localStorage.getItem('missGroupFilterCounter');
            if (saved) {
                customFilters = JSON.parse(saved);
                customFilters.forEach(filter => {
                    filter.numbers = [...new Set(filter.numbers)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
                });
            }
            if (savedCounter) filterIdCounter = parseInt(savedCounter);
            renderFiltersList();
        }

        function addToActiveFilter(number) {
            const num = parseInt(number, 10);
            if (isNaN(num)) return;
            if (customFilters.length === 0) addNewFilter();
            
            const activeFilter = customFilters[customFilters.length - 1];
            
            if (activeFilter) {
                const isUsedInOtherFilter = customFilters.some(filter => 
                    filter.id !== activeFilter.id && filter.numbers.includes(num)
                );
                if (isUsedInOtherFilter) return;
                
                const uniqueNumbers = new Set(activeFilter.numbers);
                uniqueNumbers.add(num);
                activeFilter.numbers = Array.from(uniqueNumbers).sort((a, b) => a - b);
                if (activeFilter.maxCount > 6) activeFilter.maxCount = 6;
                renderFiltersList();
            }
        }

        function addGroupToFilter(range) {
            if (customFilters.length === 0) addNewFilter();
            
            const miss = missHistory[selectedRound] || currentMiss;
            const activeFilter = customFilters[customFilters.length - 1];
            
            if (activeFilter) {
                const usedNumbers = new Set();
                customFilters.forEach(filter => {
                    if (filter.id !== activeFilter.id) {
                        filter.numbers.forEach(num => usedNumbers.add(num));
                    }
                });
                
                const uniqueNumbers = new Set(activeFilter.numbers);
                let added = false;
                for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                    if (getMissRange(miss[num]) === range && !usedNumbers.has(num)) {
                        uniqueNumbers.add(num);
                        added = true;
                    }
                }
                
                if (added) {
                    activeFilter.numbers = Array.from(uniqueNumbers).sort((a, b) => a - b);
                    if (activeFilter.maxCount > 6) activeFilter.maxCount = 6;
                    renderFiltersList();
                }
            }
        }

        function renderFiltersList() {
            const container = document.getElementById('filtersList');
            if (customFilters.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<p class="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center py-4">+ 버튼을 눌러 필터를 추가하세요</p>';
                return;
            }
            const miss = missHistory[selectedRound] || currentMiss;

            let html = '';
            customFilters.forEach(filter => {
                const displayNumbers = [...new Set(filter.numbers)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
                html += `
                    <div class="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-4">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1 flex-wrap">
                                <input type="checkbox" ${filter.enabled ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleFilter(${filter.id}); renderFiltersList();" class="w-4 h-4 text-blue-600 rounded flex-shrink-0">
                                <input type="text" value="${filter.name}" onchange="updateFilterName(${filter.id}, this.value)" class="px-2 py-1 text-sm font-semibold border border-gray-300 rounded" style="width: 110px;">
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs">
                                    <span class="text-gray-500">구간:</span>
                                    <input type="number" min="0" max="6" value="${filter.minCount || 0}" onchange="updateFilterMinMax(${filter.id}, 'min', this.value); renderFiltersList();" class="px-1 py-0.5 text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded w-10 text-center">
                                    <span class="text-gray-400">~</span>
                                    <input type="number" min="0" max="6" value="${filter.maxCount === undefined ? 6 : filter.maxCount}" onchange="updateFilterMinMax(${filter.id}, 'max', this.value); renderFiltersList();" class="px-1 py-0.5 text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded w-10 text-center">
                                </div>
                                <button onclick="saveFilters(); alert('필터가 저장되었습니다')" class="p-1 text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700 transition-colors" title="저장">
                                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg">save</span>
                                </button>
                                <button onclick="deleteFilter(${filter.id})" class="p-1 text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 transition-colors" title="삭제">
                                    <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-lg">delete</span>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="drop-zone ${filter.enabled ? '' : 'opacity-50'}" ondrop="dropNumber(event, ${filter.id})" ondragover="allowDrop(event)" ondragleave="dragLeave(event)">
                            ${displayNumbers.length === 0 
                                ? '<p class="text-sm text-gray-400 text-center">번호를 드래그하거나 더블클릭하여 추가하세요</p>' 
                                : displayNumbers.map(n => {
                                    const missCount = miss[n];
                                    const className = getMissClass(missCount);
                                    return `<span class="filter-number ${className}" onclick="removeNumberFromFilter(${filter.id}, ${n})">${n}</span>`;
                                }).join('')
                            }
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            });
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function updateSelectedRoundDisplay() {
            const miss = missHistory[selectedRound];
            if (!miss) return;
            
            const latestRoundInDB = allData[0].round;
            const isPast = selectedRound <= latestRoundInDB;
            const isDraggable = !isPast; 
            
            const currentDraw = isPast ? allData.find(d => d.round === selectedRound) : null;
            const groups = { 1: [], 2: [], 3: [], 4: [] };
            
            for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                const range = getMissRange(miss[num]);
                groups[range].push({ num, miss: miss[num] });
            }
            
            let html = '<div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-4">';

            [
                { range: 1, color: '#E64A19', label: '1-5회' },
                { range: 2, color: '#4B5563', label: '6-10회' },
                { range: 3, color: '#16A34A', label: '11-15회' },
                { range: 4, color: '#1A6DFF', label: '16+회' }
            ].forEach(({ range, color, label }) => {
                const count = groups[range].length;
                html += `
                    <div class="space-y-2">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                            <h4 class="text-sm font-semibold cursor-pointer hover:opacity-70" style="color: ${color};" onclick="addGroupToFilter(${range})">${label}</h4>
                            <span class="text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-700">${count}개</span>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                            ${groups[range].sort((a, b) => a.num - b.num).map(item => {
                                const isWinning = isPast && currentDraw && currentDraw.numbers.includes(item.num);
                                const className = isWinning ? 'winning-bg' : getMissClass(item.miss);
                                const draggable = isDraggable ? `draggable="true" ondragstart="dragStart(event, ${item.num})" ondragend="dragEnd(event)"` : '';
                                const dblclick = isDraggable ? `ondblclick="addToActiveFilter(${item.num})"` : '';
                                return `<span class="lotto-number ${className}" ${draggable} ${dblclick} title="${item.num}번: ${item.miss}회 미출현">${item.num}</span>`;
                            }).join('')}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
            });
            
            html += '</div>';
            document.getElementById('selectedRoundMiss').innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderMissGroupTable() {
            const groups = { 1: [], 2: [], 3: [], 4: [] };
            for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                const range = getMissRange(currentMiss[num]);
                groups[range].push(num);
            }
            
            // 통계 카드 업데이트
            document.getElementById('cardCount1').textContent = groups[1].length + '개';
            document.getElementById('cardCount2').textContent = groups[2].length + '개';
            document.getElementById('cardCount3').textContent = groups[3].length + '개';
            document.getElementById('cardCount4').textContent = groups[4].length + '개';
            
            // 기존 테이블 카운트 업데이트
            document.getElementById('count1Total').textContent = groups[1].length;
            document.getElementById('count2Total').textContent = groups[2].length;
            document.getElementById('count3Total').textContent = groups[3].length;
            document.getElementById('count4Total').textContent = groups[4].length;
            
            const maxMisses = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
            for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                const range = getMissRange(currentMiss[num]);
                maxMisses[range] = Math.max(maxMisses[range], currentMiss[num]);
            }
            
            let html = '';
            const maxRows = Math.max(...Object.values(maxMisses));
            
            for (let miss = maxRows; miss >= 0; miss--) {
                const row1 = groups[1].filter(n => currentMiss[n] === miss);
                const row2 = groups[2].filter(n => currentMiss[n] === miss);
                const row3 = groups[3].filter(n => currentMiss[n] === miss);
                const row4 = groups[4].filter(n => currentMiss[n] === miss);
                
                if (row1.length || row2.length || row3.length || row4.length) {
                    html += `
                        <tr class="hover:bg-gray-50">
                            <td class="py-2 px-6 text-center font-semibold text-gray-900 border border-gray-200" style="font-size: 12px;">${miss}회</td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-8 text-center border border-gray-200" style="font-size: 11px;">
                                ${row1.length ? row1.sort((a, b) => a - b).join(', ') : '-'}
                            </td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-6 text-center border border-gray-200" style="font-size: 11px;">
                                ${row2.length ? row2.sort((a, b) => a - b).join(', ') : '-'}
                            </td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-6 text-center border border-gray-200" style="font-size: 11px;">
                                ${row3.length ? row3.sort((a, b) => a - b).join(', ') : '-'}
                            </td>
                            <td class="py-2 px-6 text-center border border-gray-200" style="font-size: 11px;">
                                ${row4.length ? row4.sort((a, b) => a - b).join(', ') : '-'}
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    `;
                }
            }
            document.getElementById('missGroupTableBody').innerHTML = html;
        }

        // ... 나머지 함수들 (updateFilterMaxValues, updateDisplayRange, renderDrawsList, refreshAIAnalysis, highlightRows) 그대로 유지 ...
        
        function updateFilterMaxValues() {
            for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
                document.getElementById(`range${i}Min`).max = 6;
                document.getElementById(`range${i}Max`).max = 6;
                if(document.getElementById(`range${i}Max`).value === "" || document.getElementById(`range${i}Max`).value === "0") {
                    document.getElementById(`range${i}Max`).value = 6;
                }
            }
        }

        function updateDisplayRange(value) {
            displayLimit = parseInt(value);
            document.getElementById('rangeLabel').textContent = value == allData.length ? '전체' : value + '회';
            updateSliderBackground(value);
            renderDrawsList();
        }

        function updateSliderBackground(value) {
            const total = allData.length;
            const slider = document.getElementById('displayRange');
            if (slider) {
                const percent = ((value - 10) / (total - 10)) * 100;
                slider.style.setProperty('--value-percent', percent + '%');
            }
        }
        
        // 🔥 추가된 함수: 셀 하이라이트 토글
        function toggleStatHighlight(type, val) {
            if (selectedCellHighlight.type === type && selectedCellHighlight.val === val) {
                selectedCellHighlight = { type: null, val: null };
            } else {
                selectedCellHighlight = { type, val };
            }
            renderDrawsList();
        }

        function renderDrawsList() {
            let filteredData = [...allData];
            
            if (useRangeFilter) {
                const r1Min = parseInt(document.getElementById('range1Min').value);
                const r1Max = parseInt(document.getElementById('range1Max').value);
                const r2Min = parseInt(document.getElementById('range2Min').value);
                const r2Max = parseInt(document.getElementById('range2Max').value);
                const r3Min = parseInt(document.getElementById('range3Min').value);
                const r3Max = parseInt(document.getElementById('range3Max').value);
                const r4Min = parseInt(document.getElementById('range4Min').value);
                const r4Max = parseInt(document.getElementById('range4Max').value);
                
                filteredData = filteredData.filter(draw => {
                    const miss = missHistory[draw.round];
                    if (!miss) return false;
                    
                    const hitCounts = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
                    draw.numbers.forEach(num => {
                        const range = getMissRange(miss[num]);
                        hitCounts[range]++;
                    });
                    
                    return hitCounts[1] >= r1Min && hitCounts[1] <= r1Max &&
                           hitCounts[2] >= r2Min && hitCounts[2] <= r2Max &&
                           hitCounts[3] >= r3Min && hitCounts[3] <= r3Max &&
                           hitCounts[4] >= r4Min && hitCounts[4] <= r4Max;
                });
            }
            
            let html = '';
            const displayData = filteredData.slice(0, displayLimit);
            
            if (displayData.length === 0) {
                html = `
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="10" class="py-12 text-center text-gray-500">
                            <div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                                <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-5xl text-gray-300">inbox</span>
                                <p class="text-sm">필터 조건에 맞는 데이터가 없습니다</p>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            } else {
                const container = document.getElementById('drawsList');
                container.innerHTML = ''; 

                displayData.forEach((draw, index) => {
                    const miss = missHistory[draw.round];
                    if (!miss) return;
                    
                    const missCounts = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
                    for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                        if (!draw.numbers.includes(num)) {
                            const range = getMissRange(miss[num]);
                            missCounts[range]++;
                        }
                    }
                    
                    const hitCounts = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
                    draw.numbers.forEach(num => {
                        const range = getMissRange(miss[num]);
                        hitCounts[range]++;
                    });
                    
                    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                    // 🔥 수정: 행 높이 줄임 (py-3 -> py-1.5)
                    tr.className = 'border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer transition-colors';
                    tr.onclick = () => highlightRows(index);

                    const numberBalls = draw.numbers.map(num => {
                        const missCount = miss[num];
                        const className = getMissClass(missCount);
                        return `<div class="flex flex-col items-center">
                            <span class="lotto-number ${className}" title="${num}번: ${missCount}회 미출현">${num}</span>
                            <span class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">${missCount}</span>
                        </div>`;
                    }).join('');
                    
                    // 🔥 셀 하이라이트 스타일 도우미
                    const getHighlightClass = (type, val) => {
                        if (selectedCellHighlight.type === type && selectedCellHighlight.val === val) {
                            return 'cell-highlight-active';
                        }
                        return 'cursor-pointer';
                    };
                    const getTextColor = (type, val, defaultColor) => {
                         if (selectedCellHighlight.type === type && selectedCellHighlight.val === val) return '';
                         return `style="color: ${defaultColor};"`;
                    };

                    // 🔥 수정: 폰트 크기 줄임 (text-sm, text-xs), 셀 하이라이트 이벤트 추가
                    tr.innerHTML = `
                        <td class="py-2 px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-900 sticky left-0 bg-white">${draw.round}회</td>
                        <td class="py-2 px-4 sticky bg-white border-r-2 border-gray-300" style="left: 80px;">
                            <div class="flex gap-1 justify-center">${numberBalls}</div>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-sm font-bold" style="color: #E64A19;">${missCounts[1]}</td>
                        <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-sm font-bold" style="color: #4B5563;">${missCounts[2]}</td>
                        <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-sm font-bold" style="color: #16A34A;">${missCounts[3]}</td>
                        <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-sm font-bold" style="color: #1A6DFF;">${missCounts[4]}</td>
                        
                        <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-sm font-bold border-l-2 border-gray-300">
                            <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleStatHighlight('hit1', ${hitCounts[1]})" 
                                  class="${getHighlightClass('hit1', hitCounts[1])}" ${getTextColor('hit1', hitCounts[1], '#E64A19')}>
                                ${hitCounts[1]}
                            </span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-sm font-bold">
                            <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleStatHighlight('hit2', ${hitCounts[2]})" 
                                  class="${getHighlightClass('hit2', hitCounts[2])}" ${getTextColor('hit2', hitCounts[2], '#4B5563')}>
                                ${hitCounts[2]}
                            </span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-sm font-bold">
                            <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleStatHighlight('hit3', ${hitCounts[3]})" 
                                  class="${getHighlightClass('hit3', hitCounts[3])}" ${getTextColor('hit3', hitCounts[3], '#16A34A')}>
                                ${hitCounts[3]}
                            </span>
                        </td>
                        <td class="py-2 px-3 text-center text-sm font-bold">
                            <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleStatHighlight('hit4', ${hitCounts[4]})" 
                                  class="${getHighlightClass('hit4', hitCounts[4])}" ${getTextColor('hit4', hitCounts[4], '#1A6DFF')}>
                                ${hitCounts[4]}
                            </span>
                        </td>
                    `;
                    container.appendChild(tr);
                });
            }
        }

        function highlightRows(startIndex) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.row-highlight').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('row-highlight'));
            const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#drawsList > tr');
            
            for(let i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
                if (startIndex + i < rows.length) {
                    rows[startIndex + i].classList.add('row-highlight');
                }
            }
        }

        // 🔹 [AI 분석 엔진 - 미출현 전용 로직 개선]
        // common_v2.js의 AIAnalysis 엔진을 사용하여 hot_cold.html과 동일한 디자인 적용
        async function refreshAIAnalysis() {
            if (allData.length === 0) return;

            // 1. 데이터 준비
            const subjectData = allData[0];
            const subjectRound = subjectData.round;
            const targetRound = subjectRound + 1;

            // 미출현 통계 요약 생성
            const missValues = Object.values(currentMiss);
            const maxMiss = Math.max(...missValues);
            const maxMissNum = Object.keys(currentMiss).find(k => currentMiss[k] === maxMiss);
            const groups = { 1: [], 2: [], 3: [], 4: [] };
            for (let num = 1; num <= 45; num++) {
                const range = getMissRange(currentMiss[num]);
                groups[range].push(num);
            }
            
            // 2. 헤더 정보 업데이트
            const targetEl = document.getElementById('aiTargetInfo');
            if(targetEl) targetEl.textContent = `Target ${targetRound}회차_based on ${subjectRound}회차`;

            // 3. AI 엔진 실행 (hot_cold.html과 동일한 방식)
            await window.AIAnalysis.executeAnalysis({
                containerId: 'aiAnalysisContent', // 결과를 뿌릴 div ID
                analysisType: '미출현',             // 프롬프트 타입
                subjectRound: subjectRound,
                targetRound: targetRound,
                // AI에게 보낼 맞춤형 데이터
                customData: `
# [미출현 번호 심층 분석 데이터]
- 기준 회차: ${subjectRound}회
- 최장기 미출현 번호: ${maxMissNum}번 (현재 ${maxMiss}회 연속 미출현)
- 구간별 현황:
  * 1~5회(최근): ${groups[1].length}개
  * 6~10회(중간): ${groups[2].length}개
  * 11~15회(장기): ${groups[3].length}개
  * 16회+(극장기): ${groups[4].length}개
                `,
                // AI에게 요청할 분석 규칙
                customRules: `
# [분석 요구사항]
1. 위험도 분석 (Risk): 장기 미출현 번호(Cold Number)에 대한 과도한 베팅의 위험성 분석.
2. 반전 신호 (Turnaround): 통계적으로 장기 미출현 번호가 출현할 임계점 분석.
3. 갭 분석 (Gap Analysis): 특정 구간(예: 6~10회)에 몰려있는 번호들의 출현 가능성 진단.
4. 추천 (Recommendation): 다음 회차 조합 시 포함해야 할 미출현 번호의 적정 개수와 전략 제안.
                `
            });
        }

        function updateAnalysis() {}

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            loadData();
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

        // 전역 변수 설정
        let allDrawData = [];
        let currentRange = 100;
        let chartTab = 'rate'; // 'rate' or 'trend'
        let trendChartInstance = null;
        
        // 초기 상태: 선택된 배수 없음 (종합 분석 모드)
        let selectedGraphType = null; 

        // 셀 하이라이트 상태 관리 (컬럼명, 값)
        let selectedHighlight = { col: null, val: null };

        // 배수 정의
        const multipleDefinitions = {
            '3배수': [3, 6, 9, 12, 15, 18, 21, 24, 27, 30, 33, 36, 39, 42, 45],
            '4배수': [4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 36, 40, 44],
            '5배수': [5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35, 40, 45],
            '3·4배수': [12, 24, 36],
            '3·5배수': [15, 30, 45],
            '4·5배수': [20, 40],
            '배수외': [1, 2, 7, 11, 13, 14, 17, 19, 22, 23, 26, 29, 31, 34, 37, 38, 41, 43]
        };

        // 색상 정의
        const multipleColors = {
            '3배수': '#4F6AFF',
            '4배수': '#10B981',
            '5배수': '#EC4899',
            '3·4배수': '#8B5CF6',

        };

        // 필터 상태
        let filterState = {}; 

        // [추가됨] 필터 설정 저장 함수
        function saveFilterSettings() {
            const filters = {};
            Object.keys(multipleDefinitions).forEach(type => {
                const minInput = document.getElementById(`min_${type}`);
                const maxInput = document.getElementById(`max_${type}`);
                if (minInput && maxInput) {
                    filters[type] = {
                        min: parseInt(minInput.value),
                        max: parseInt(maxInput.value)
                    };
                }
            });

            const data = {
                filters: filters,
                enabled: document.getElementById('applyFilterCheck').checked
            };

            if (window.Utils && window.Utils.saveFilter) {
                window.Utils.saveFilter('multiple_filter', data);
            }
        }

        // [추가됨] 필터 설정 불러오기 함수
        function loadFilterSettings() {
            if (window.Utils && window.Utils.loadFilter) {
                const saved = window.Utils.loadFilter('multiple_filter');
                if (saved) {
                    if (saved.filters) {
                        Object.keys(saved.filters).forEach(type => {
                            const minInput = document.getElementById(`min_${type}`);
                            const maxInput = document.getElementById(`max_${type}`);
                            if (minInput && maxInput && saved.filters[type]) {
                                minInput.value = saved.filters[type].min;
                                maxInput.value = saved.filters[type].max;
                            }
                        });
                    }
                    if (saved.enabled !== undefined) {
                        document.getElementById('applyFilterCheck').checked = saved.enabled;
                    }
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

                        <div class="space-y-6" id="filterContainer">
                            </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-gray-100">
                            <label class="relative inline-flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                <input type="checkbox" id="applyFilterCheck" onchange="applyFilter()" class="sr-only peer" checked>
                                <div class="w-11 h-6 bg-gray-200 rounded-full peer peer-checked:after:translate-x-full after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:left-[2px] after:bg-white after:rounded-full after:h-5 after:w-5 after:transition-all peer-checked:bg-blue-500"></div>
                            </label>
                            <span class="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">필터 적용 (조합기 연동)</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
                        <div class="p-6 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
                            <div>
                                <h3 class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">회차별 당첨번호 및 배수 현황</h3>
                                <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">
                                    최근 <span id="displayedCount" class="font-bold text-blue-600">100</span>회차 표시
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        
                        <div class="overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar" style="max-height: 600px;">
                            <table class="w-full text-sm text-left">
                                <thead class="bg-[#1e293b] text-white font-semibold sticky top-0 z-10">
                                    <tr>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-4 text-center w-20 sticky left-0 bg-[#1e293b] z-20">회차</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-2 text-center sticky bg-[#1e293b] z-20 w-[260px]" style="left: 80px;">당첨번호</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-2 text-center text-xs w-24">3배수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-2 text-center text-xs w-24">4배수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-2 text-center text-xs w-24">5배수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-2 text-center text-xs w-24">3·4배수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-2 text-center text-xs w-24">3·5배수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-2 text-center text-xs w-24">4·5배수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-2 text-center text-xs w-24">배수외</th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody id="drawsList"></tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </main>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function() {
            try {
                // Supabase에서 데이터 로드
                const { data, error } = await supabaseClient
                    .from('lotto_draws') 
                    .select('*')
                    .order('round', { ascending: false })
                    .range(0, 5000); 

                if (error) throw error;
                
                if (!data || data.length === 0) {
                    document.getElementById('drawsList').innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="9" class="p-10 text-center">데이터가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
                    return;
                }

                processData(data);
                
                // 초기 설정
                const totalCount = allDrawData.length;
                const rangeSlider = document.getElementById('rangeSlider');
                rangeSlider.max = totalCount;
                document.getElementById('totalRangeDisplay').textContent = `최대 ${totalCount.toLocaleString()}회`;
                document.getElementById('preset1203').setAttribute('onclick', `setRange(${totalCount})`);
                
                initFilters(); // 필터 생성
                loadFilterSettings(); // [추가됨] 저장된 필터 불러오기
                initStatCards();
                
                updateRange(currentRange); 
                
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('데이터 로드 실패:', err);
                document.getElementById('drawsList').innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="9" class="p-10 text-center text-red-500">데이터 로드 실패<br>${err.message}</td></tr>`;
            }
        });

        function calculateMultiplesCounts(numbers) {
            const counts = {};
            Object.keys(multipleDefinitions).forEach(key => {
                counts[key] = numbers.filter(n => multipleDefinitions[key].includes(n)).length;
            });
            return counts;
        }

        function processData(rawData) {
            allDrawData = rawData.map(draw => {
                const counts = calculateMultiplesCounts(draw.numbers);
                return { ...draw, counts };
            });
        }

        // --- 필터 및 통계 초기화 ---

        function initFilters() {
            const recent10 = allDrawData.slice(0, 10);
            const container = document.getElementById('filterContainer');
            container.innerHTML = '';

            Object.keys(multipleDefinitions).forEach(type => {
                const counts = recent10.map(d => d.counts[type]);
                const minVal = Math.min(...counts);
                const maxVal = Math.max(...counts);
                
                filterState[type] = { min: minVal, max: maxVal };

                const numbersHtml = multipleDefinitions[type].map(n => 
                    `<span class="lotto-ball-mini" style="background-color: ${multipleColors[type]};">${n}</span>`
                ).join('');

                const gapClass = type === '배수외' ? 'gap-0.5' : 'gap-1';

                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.className = 'border-b border-gray-100 pb-4 last:border-0';
                
                // [수정됨] input에 id 추가 및 onchange 이벤트 연결
                div.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                        <div class="flex-1">
                            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                                <span class="font-bold text-gray-700 w-16">${type}</span>
                                <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">(${multipleDefinitions[type].length}개)</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="flex flex-wrap ${gapClass}">
                                ${numbersHtml}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span class="text-sm text-gray-500">최소</span>
                            <input type="number" id="min_${type}" onchange="applyFilter()" min="0" max="6" value="${minVal}" class="w-20 h-9 text-center border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-900 font-bold focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500">
                            <span class="text-gray-400">~</span>
                            <span class="text-sm text-gray-500">최대</span>
                            <input type="number" id="max_${type}" onchange="applyFilter()" min="0" max="6" value="${maxVal}" class="w-20 h-9 text-center border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-900 font-bold focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500">
                        </div>
                    </div>
                `;
                container.appendChild(div);
            });
        }

        function initStatCards() {
            const container = document.getElementById('statCards');
            container.innerHTML = '';
            
            Object.keys(multipleDefinitions).forEach(type => {
                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.className = 'bg-white rounded-xl p-3 border border-gray-200 relative overflow-hidden group hover:shadow-md transition-all text-center cursor-pointer stat-card-transition';
                div.onclick = () => toggleStatCard(type, div);
                div.id = `card-${type}`;
                
                div.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 mb-1">${type}</div>
                    <p id="stat_${type}" class="text-lg font-bold text-gray-900">-</p>
                `;
                container.appendChild(div);
            });
        }

        function toggleStatCard(type, element) {
            if (selectedGraphType === type) {
                selectedGraphType = null; 
            } else {
                selectedGraphType = type; 
            }
            updateStatCardStyles();
            if (selectedGraphType) {
                document.getElementById('graphTypeSelect').value = selectedGraphType;
            }
            const displayData = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            renderDrawsList(displayData);
            updateCharts(displayData);
            refreshAIAnalysis();
        }

        function updateStatCardStyles() {
            Object.keys(multipleDefinitions).forEach(type => {
                const card = document.getElementById(`card-${type}`);
                if (!card) return;

                if (selectedGraphType === type) {
                    card.classList.add('stat-card-active');
                    card.style.backgroundColor = multipleColors[type];
                    card.querySelector('p').classList.remove('text-gray-900');
                    card.querySelector('p').classList.add('text-white');
                    card.querySelector('.text-xs').classList.remove('text-gray-500');
                    card.querySelector('.text-xs').classList.add('text-white');
                } else {
                    card.classList.remove('stat-card-active');
                    card.style.backgroundColor = 'white';
                    card.querySelector('p').classList.remove('text-white');
                    card.querySelector('p').classList.add('text-gray-900');
                    card.querySelector('.text-xs').classList.remove('text-white');
                    card.querySelector('.text-xs').classList.add('text-gray-500');
                }
            });
        }

        // --- 업데이트 및 렌더링 ---

        function updateRange(value) {
            currentRange = parseInt(value);
            document.getElementById('rangeDisplay').textContent = value + '회';
            updateSliderBackground(value);
            updatePresetButtons(currentRange);
            
            const displayData = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            updateStats(displayData);
            renderDrawsList(displayData);
            updateCharts(displayData);
            refreshAIAnalysis();
        }

        function updateStats(data) {
            const total = data.length;
            if (total === 0) return;

            Object.keys(multipleDefinitions).forEach(type => {
                const sum = data.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr.counts[type], 0);
                const avg = (sum / total).toFixed(1);
                document.getElementById(`stat_${type}`).textContent = avg + '개';
            });
        }

        // 하이라이트 토글 함수 (동일 값 다크그레이 하이라이트)
        function toggleCellHighlight(col, val) {
            if (selectedHighlight.col === col && selectedHighlight.val === val) {
                selectedHighlight = { col: null, val: null };
            } else {
                selectedHighlight = { col: col, val: val };
            }
            const displayData = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            renderDrawsList(displayData);
        }

        function renderDrawsList(draws) {
            const container = document.getElementById('drawsList');
            container.innerHTML = '';
            document.getElementById('displayedCount').textContent = draws.length;

            draws.forEach((draw, index) => {
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                tr.className = 'border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer transition-colors';
                tr.onclick = () => highlightRows(index);

                // 당첨번호 공 생성
                const ballsHtml = draw.numbers.map(num => {
                    let className = 'ball-normal'; // common.css
                    let style = '';
                    
                    if (selectedGraphType && multipleDefinitions[selectedGraphType].includes(num)) {
                        className = 'ball-custom';
                        style = `background-color: ${multipleColors[selectedGraphType]}; border-color: ${multipleColors[selectedGraphType]};`;
                    }
                    
                    return `<span class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full ${className} text-xs mx-0.5" style="${style}">${String(num).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                }).join('');

                // 배수 카운트 열 생성
                let countsHtml = '';
                Object.keys(multipleDefinitions).forEach(type => {
                    const count = draw.counts[type];
                    let badgeClass = 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'; 
                    
                    // 값에 따른 기본 배경색
                    if (count === 1) badgeClass = 'bg-pink-100 text-pink-700';
                    else if (count === 2) badgeClass = 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
                    else if (count === 3) badgeClass = 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700';
                    else if (count === 4) badgeClass = 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-700';
                    else if (count >= 5) badgeClass = 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700';

                    // 하이라이트 적용 (동일 컬럼, 동일 값)
                    if (selectedHighlight.col === type && selectedHighlight.val === count) {
                        badgeClass = 'val-highlight'; // 다크그레이 배경 + 흰색 글씨
                    }
                    
                    // 세로선(border-r) 제거
                    countsHtml += `<td class="py-3 px-2 text-center"><span class="count-badge ${badgeClass}" onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCellHighlight('${type}', ${count})">${count}</span></td>`;
                });

                tr.innerHTML = `
                    <td class="py-3 px-4 text-center text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 sticky left-0 bg-inherit">${draw.round}회</td>
                    <td class="py-3 px-2 text-center sticky bg-inherit" style="left: 80px;"><div class="flex items-center justify-center">${ballsHtml}</div></td>
                    ${countsHtml}
                `;
                container.appendChild(tr);
            });
        }

        function highlightRows(startIndex) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.row-highlight').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('row-highlight'));
            const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#drawsList > tr');
            for(let i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
                if (startIndex + i < rows.length) {
                    rows[startIndex + i].classList.add('row-highlight');
                }
            }
        }

        // --- 차트 ---

        function setChartTab(tab) {
            chartTab = tab;
            const btnRate = document.getElementById('tabRate');
            const btnTrend = document.getElementById('tabTrend');
            
            if (tab === 'rate') {
                btnRate.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors';
                btnTrend.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 rounded-lg transition-colors';
                document.getElementById('rateChartContainer').classList.remove('hidden');
                document.getElementById('rateChartContainer').classList.add('flex');
                document.getElementById('trendChartContainer').classList.add('hidden');
            } else {
                btnRate.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 rounded-lg transition-colors';
                btnTrend.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors';
                document.getElementById('rateChartContainer').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('rateChartContainer').classList.remove('flex');
                document.getElementById('trendChartContainer').classList.remove('hidden');
                updateTrendChart();
            }
        }

        function changeGraphType(type) {
            if (selectedGraphType !== type) {
                selectedGraphType = type;
                updateStatCardStyles(); 
                
                const displayData = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
                renderDrawsList(displayData);
                updateCharts(displayData);
                refreshAIAnalysis();
            }
        }

        function updateCharts(data) {
            updateRateChart(data);
            if (chartTab === 'trend') updateTrendChart();
        }

        function updateRateChart(data) {
            const total = data.length;
            const graphTarget = selectedGraphType || '3배수'; 
            
            const counts = [0,0,0,0,0,0,0];
            data.forEach(d => {
                const val = d.counts[graphTarget];
                if (val !== undefined && val <= 6) counts[val]++;
            });
            
            const maxPct = Math.max(...counts.map(c => c / total * 100));
            const barColor = multipleColors[graphTarget] || '#3B82F6';
            
            let html = '';
            for (let i = 0; i <= 6; i++) {
                const pct = (counts[i] / total * 100).toFixed(1);
                const height = maxPct > 0 ? (counts[i] / total * 100 / maxPct * 85) : 0; 
                
                html += `<div class="flex flex-col items-center flex-1 h-full justify-end group">
                    <div class="flex-1 w-full px-1 sm:px-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-end">
                        <span class="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-1 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">${pct}%</span>
                        <div class="w-full max-w-[40px] rounded-t-lg transition-all duration-500 relative group-hover:opacity-80" style="height: ${Math.max(height, 2)}%; background-color: ${barColor}"></div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2 font-medium">${i}개</span>
                </div>`;
            }
            document.getElementById('rateChartContainer').innerHTML = html;
        }

        function updateTrendChart() {
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange).reverse();
            const labels = data.map(d => d.round + '회');
            const graphTarget = selectedGraphType || '3배수';
            const values = data.map(d => d.counts[graphTarget]);
            const color = multipleColors[graphTarget] || '#3B82F6';

            if (trendChartInstance) trendChartInstance.destroy();
            const ctx = document.getElementById('trendChart').getContext('2d');
            
            trendChartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: labels,
                    datasets: [{
                        label: graphTarget,
                        data: values,
                        borderColor: color,
                        backgroundColor: color + '20', 
                        borderWidth: 2,
                        tension: 0.3,
                        pointRadius: 2,
                        fill: true
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                    scales: {
                        y: { 
                            beginAtZero: true, 
                            max: 6, 
                            ticks: { stepSize: 1 },
                            grid: { display: false } 
                        },
                        x: { 
                            display: true, 
                            ticks: { maxTicksLimit: 10 },
                            grid: { display: false } 
                        }
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        // --- 공통 유틸리티 ---
        function updateSliderBackground(value) {
            const total = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const slider = document.getElementById('rangeSlider');
            if (slider) {
                const percent = ((value - 10) / (total - 10)) * 100;
                slider.style.setProperty('--value-percent', percent + '%');
            }
        }

        function setRange(value) {
            const maxVal = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const safeValue = value > maxVal ? maxVal : value;
            document.getElementById('rangeSlider').value = safeValue;
            updateRange(safeValue);
        }

        function updatePresetButtons(value) {
            const maxVal = allDrawData.length;
            const buttons = { 50: 'preset50', 100: 'preset100', 500: 'preset500', 1203: 'preset1203' };
            Object.keys(buttons).forEach(key => {
                const btn = document.getElementById(buttons[key]);
                const targetValue = key === '1203' ? maxVal : parseInt(key);
                if (targetValue === value) btn.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors';
                else btn.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 rounded-lg transition-colors';
            });
        }

        // [수정됨] 필터 적용 시마다 저장 함수 호출
        function applyFilter() {
            console.log("필터 적용 (조합기 연동 준비)");
            saveFilterSettings(); // 저장 호출 추가
        }

        // ==========================================================
        // [AI 분석 엔진] common_v2.js 기반 자동 분석
        // ==========================================================
        async function refreshAIAnalysis() {
            if (!allDrawData || allDrawData.length === 0) return;

            const subjectData = allDrawData[0];
            const subjectRound = subjectData.round;
            const targetRound = subjectRound + 1;
            
            // AI 섹션 헤더 업데이트
            const targetEl = document.getElementById('aiTargetInfo');
            if(targetEl) targetEl.textContent = `Target ${targetRound}회차_based on ${subjectRound}회차`;

            // 분석 대상 (특정 배수 선택 여부 확인)
            const targetType = selectedGraphType || '전체 배수';
            let summaryData = '';
            
            if (selectedGraphType) {
                // 특정 배수 모드
                const count = subjectData.counts[selectedGraphType];
                summaryData = `- 분석 대상: ${selectedGraphType} (직전 ${count}개 출현)`;
            } else {
                // 종합 모드
                const c = subjectData.counts;
                summaryData = `- 배수별 현황: 3배수(${c['3배수']}), 4배수(${c['4배수']}), 5배수(${c['5배수']}) 등`;
            }

            // AI 실행
            await window.AIAnalysis.executeAnalysis({
                containerId: 'aiAnalysisContent',
                analysisType: '배수 패턴',
                subjectRound: subjectRound,
                targetRound: targetRound,
                customData: `
# [분석 데이터]
- 기준 회차: ${subjectRound}회
${summaryData}
- 배수 정의:
  * 3배수: 3, 6, 9... (총 15개)
  * 4배수: 4, 8, 12... (총 11개)
  * 5배수: 5, 10, 15... (총 9개)
                `,
                customRules: `
# [배수 분석 핵심 가이드]
1. 균형 이론: 특정 배수가 과다 출현(4개 이상)하면 다음 회차엔 감소할 확률이 높음.
2. 멸 구간: 특정 배수가 0개 출현(멸)했다면, 다음 회차 필출 가능성 분석.
3. 중복 구간: 공배수(3·4배수=12,24,36 등)의 흐름 체크.
4. 배수외 숫자: 배수에 속하지 않는 소수(Prime)나 기타 숫자의 비중 분석.
                `
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <style>

            border-color: #374151 !important;
        }

        /* --- 공 색상 정의 --- */
        
        /* 1. 일반 이웃수 매칭 (파란색) */
        .ball-neighbor {
            background-color: #3B82F6;
            color: white;
            border: 1px solid #2563EB;
        }
        
        /* 2. 보너스볼 이웃수 매칭 (빨간색) */
        .ball-bonus-match {
            background-color: #EF4444; /* red-500 */
            color: white;
            border: 1px solid #DC2626;
        }

        /* 3. 중복(이웃수+보볼이웃수) 매칭 (핑크색) */
        .ball-overlap-match {
            background-color: #EC4899; /* pink-500 */
            color: white;
            border: 1px solid #DB2777;
        }
        
        /* 보너스 볼 자체 스타일 */
        .ball-bonus {
            border: 1px solid #D1D5DB;
            color: #4B5563;
            background-color: #F3F4F6;
        }

        /* 배지 스타일 (당첨개수, 보볼이웃수 공통) */
        .count-badge {
            width: 28px; height: 28px; border-radius: 50%; display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
            font-weight: 700; font-size: 13px;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: all 0.2s;
        }
        .count-badge:hover {
            transform: scale(1.1);
        }

            color: white !important;
            border-color: transparent !important;

            box-shadow: 0 4px 6px -1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1), 0 2px 4px -1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
        }

                                    최근 <span id="displayedCount" class="font-bold text-blue-600">100</span>회차 표시 | 
                                    <span class="text-blue-600">파란색</span>: 이웃수 | 
                                    <span class="text-red-500">빨간색</span>: 보볼이웃수 |

                                        <th class="py-3 px-4 text-left w-[500px]">전회차 이웃수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-4 text-center w-28 whitespace-nowrap">이웃수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-4 text-center w-28 whitespace-nowrap">당첨개수</th>
                                        <th class="py-3 px-4 text-center w-28 whitespace-nowrap">보볼이웃수</th>
