        // [추가] 필터 설정 저장 함수 (Missing 패턴)
        function saveFilterSettings() {
            const activeFilters = [];
            for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
                const btn = document.getElementById(`filter-btn-${i}`);
                if (btn && btn.classList.contains('filter-btn-active')) {
                    activeFilters.push(i);
                }
            }

            const data = {
                activeCounts: activeFilters,
                enabled: document.getElementById('applyFilterCheck').checked
            };

            if (window.Utils && window.Utils.saveFilter) {
                window.Utils.saveFilter('twin_filter', data);
            }
        }

        // [추가] 필터 설정 불러오기 함수 (Missing 패턴)
        function loadFilterSettings() {
            if (window.Utils && window.Utils.loadFilter) {
                const saved = window.Utils.loadFilter('twin_filter');
                if (saved) {
                    // 1. 모든 필터 초기화
                    for(let i=0; i<=4; i++) {
                        const btn = document.getElementById(`filter-btn-${i}`);
                        if(btn) btn.classList.remove('filter-btn-active');
                        updateCardVisual(i, false);
                    }

                    // 2. 저장된 필터 적용
                    if (saved.activeCounts && Array.isArray(saved.activeCounts)) {
                        saved.activeCounts.forEach(num => {
                            const btn = document.getElementById(`filter-btn-${num}`);
                            if (btn) btn.classList.add('filter-btn-active');
                            updateCardVisual(num, true);
                        });
                    }

                    // 3. 필터 사용 여부 체크
                    if (saved.enabled !== undefined) {
                        document.getElementById('applyFilterCheck').checked = saved.enabled;
                    }
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        // 카드 시각적 상태 업데이트 헬퍼
        function updateCardVisual(num, isActive) {
            const card = document.getElementById(`card-${num}`);
            if (!card) return;
            
            const valueText = document.getElementById(`count_${num}`);
            const titleText = card.querySelector('.text-xs');

            if (isActive) {
                card.classList.remove('bg-white', 'border-gray-200');
                card.classList.add('bg-blue-600', 'border-blue-600', 'shadow-md');
                valueText.classList.remove('text-gray-900'); valueText.classList.add('text-white');
                titleText.classList.remove('text-gray-500'); titleText.classList.add('text-blue-100');
            } else {
                card.classList.remove('bg-blue-600', 'border-blue-600', 'shadow-md');
                card.classList.add('bg-white', 'border-gray-200');
                valueText.classList.remove('text-white'); valueText.classList.add('text-gray-900');
                titleText.classList.remove('text-blue-100'); titleText.classList.add('text-gray-500');
            }
        }

                 <table class="w-full border-collapse">

                            <th class="border border-gray-300 px-2 py-2"><input type="checkbox" id="exclude_33" onchange="syncExcludeFilter()" class="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 rounded focus:ring-red-500"></th>
                            <th class="border border-gray-300 px-2 py-2"><input type="checkbox" id="exclude_44" onchange="syncExcludeFilter()" class="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 rounded focus:ring-red-500"></th>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="bg-gray-100 border-b border-gray-200">

    <script>
        function toggleStatCard(count) {
            const card = document.getElementById(`card-${count}`);
            const btn = document.getElementById(`filter-btn-${count}`);
            
            // 토글 로직 (버튼과 카드 동기화)
            const isActive = card.classList.contains('bg-blue-600');
            
            if (isActive) {
                updateCardVisual(count, false);
                if (btn) btn.classList.remove('filter-btn-active');
            } else {
                updateCardVisual(count, true);
                if (btn) btn.classList.add('filter-btn-active');
            }
            applyFilter();
        }

        function toggleFilterButton(num) {
            const btn = document.getElementById(`filter-btn-${num}`);
            const isNowActive = !btn.classList.contains('filter-btn-active');
            
            if(isNowActive) btn.classList.add('filter-btn-active');
            else btn.classList.remove('filter-btn-active');

            updateCardVisual(num, isNowActive);
            applyFilter();
        }

        function syncExcludeFilter() {
            const twinNums = [11, 22, 33, 44];
            excludedNumbers.clear();
            const selectedList = [];
            twinNums.forEach(n => {
                const chk = document.getElementById(`exclude_${n}`);
                if (chk && chk.checked) {
                    excludedNumbers.add(n);
                    selectedList.push(n);
                }
            });
            const input = document.getElementById('excludeNumbersInput');
            if(input) {
                input.value = selectedList.length > 0 ? selectedList.join(', ') : '';
            }
        }

        function applyFilter() {
            const displayData = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            renderDrawsList(displayData);
            saveFilterSettings(); // [추가] 필터 적용 시 저장 호출
        }

        // 🔥 Missing.html 패턴: Promise.all + Map 캐싱 적용
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async function() {
            try {
                const client = window.supabaseClient;
                if (!client) throw new Error("Supabase Client가 초기화되지 않았습니다.");

                // 병렬 조회
                const [drawsResult, featuresResult] = await Promise.all([
                    client.from('lotto_draws') 
                          .select('*')
                          .order('round', { ascending: false })
                          .range(0, 5000),
                    // Missing 가이드에 따라 featuresMap 구성을 위한 데이터 로드
                    client.from('number_features_by_round')
                          .select('round, number') // 필요한 필드만 조회
                          .in('number', [11, 22, 33, 44]) 
                          .range(0, 10000)
                ]);

                if (drawsResult.error) throw drawsResult.error;
                
                // Map 캐싱 (Missing 패턴)
                featuresMap = new Map();
                if (featuresResult.data && featuresResult.data.length > 0) {
                    featuresResult.data.forEach(row => {
                        if (!featuresMap.has(row.round)) {
                            featuresMap.set(row.round, []);
                        }
                        featuresMap.get(row.round).push(row.number);
                    });
                }

                if (!drawsResult.data || drawsResult.data.length === 0) {
                    document.getElementById('drawsList').innerHTML = '<div class="p-10 text-center text-gray-500">데이터가 없습니다.</div>';
                    document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').style.display = 'none';
                    return;
                }

                // processData 로직 실행
                processData(drawsResult.data);
                
                // UI 초기화
                const totalCount = allDrawData.length;
                const rangeSlider = document.getElementById('rangeSlider');
                rangeSlider.max = totalCount;
                
                document.getElementById('totalRangeDisplay').textContent = `최대 ${totalCount.toLocaleString()}회`;
                document.getElementById('preset1203').setAttribute('onclick', `setRange(${totalCount})`);
                document.getElementById('preset1203').innerText = "전체";
                
                initChart();
                updateStatistics(currentRange);
                renderDrawsList(allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange));
                updateSliderBackground(currentRange);
                
                // 필터 초기화: 저장된 값이 있으면 로드, 없으면 기본 계산값 사용
                if (!loadFilterSettings()) {
                    initFilterDefaults();
                }

                // 로딩 종료 및 AI 실행
                document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').style.display = 'none';
                await refreshAIAnalysis();
                
            } catch (err) {
                console.error('데이터 로드 실패:', err);
                const overlay = document.getElementById('loadingOverlay');
                overlay.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="text-center text-red-600">
                        <p class="font-bold mb-2">데이터 로드 실패</p>
                        <p class="text-sm">${err.message}</p>
                        <button onclick="location.reload()" class="mt-4 px-4 py-2 bg-gray-200 rounded text-sm">새로고침</button>
                    </div>`;
            }
        });

        // 데이터 가공 로직 분리 (Missing 패턴)
        function processData(rawData) {
            allDrawData = rawData.map(item => {
                const numbers = item.numbers || [];
                let twinCount;

                // 캐시 히트 시도
                if (featuresMap.has(item.round)) {
                    // DB에서 가져온 값이 있으면 그 개수를 사용
                    const cachedTwins = featuresMap.get(item.round);
                    twinCount = cachedTwins.length;
                } else {
                    // Fallback: JS 계산
                    twinCount = numbers.filter(n => isTwin(n)).length;
                }

                const nonTwinCount = 6 - twinCount; 
                return { ...item, numbers: numbers, twinCount: twinCount, nonTwinCount: nonTwinCount };
            });
        }

        function initFilterDefaults() {
            if (allDrawData.length < 10) return;
            const recent10 = allDrawData.slice(0, 10);
            
            let minNonTwin = 6, maxNonTwin = 0;
            recent10.forEach(d => {
                if (d.nonTwinCount < minNonTwin) minNonTwin = d.nonTwinCount;
                if (d.nonTwinCount > maxNonTwin) maxNonTwin = d.nonTwinCount;
            });

            const minTwin = 6 - maxNonTwin;
            const maxTwin = 6 - minNonTwin;

            // 초기화
            for(let i=0; i<=4; i++) {
                const btn = document.getElementById(`filter-btn-${i}`);
                if(btn) btn.classList.remove('filter-btn-active');
                updateCardVisual(i, false);
            }

            // 추천 범위 활성화
            for (let i = minTwin; i <= maxTwin; i++) {
                const btn = document.getElementById(`filter-btn-${i}`);
                if (btn) btn.classList.add('filter-btn-active');
                updateCardVisual(i, true);
            }
            applyFilter();
        }
        
        function updateStatistics(range) {
            if (!allDrawData.length) return;
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, range);
            
            const countMap = {0:0, 1:0, 2:0, 3:0, 4:0};
            data.forEach(d => {
                if (countMap[d.twinCount] !== undefined) countMap[d.twinCount]++;
            });
            
            for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
                const percent = (countMap[i] / data.length * 100).toFixed(1);
                document.getElementById(`count_${i}`).textContent = percent + '%';
            }
        }
        
        function highlightRows(startIndex) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.row-highlight').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('row-highlight'));
            const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#drawsList > div');
            
            for(let i = 0; i < 11; i++) {
                if (startIndex + i < rows.length) {
                    rows[startIndex + i].classList.add('row-highlight');
                }
            }
        }

        function toggleCountHighlight(val) {
            if (selectedCountValue === val) selectedCountValue = null;
            else selectedCountValue = val;
            renderDrawsList(allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange));
        }

        function renderDrawsList(draws) {
            const container = document.getElementById('drawsList');
            container.innerHTML = '';

            if (draws.length === 0) {
                container.innerHTML = '<div class="p-4 text-center text-gray-500">표시할 데이터가 없습니다.</div>';
                return;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('displayedCount').textContent = draws.length;

            draws.forEach((draw, index) => {
                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.onclick = () => highlightRows(index);
                div.className = 'flex items-center gap-4 py-3 px-0 hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors border-b border-gray-100 cursor-pointer text-center';
                
                let countClass = "flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer transition-all duration-200 ";
                
                if (selectedCountValue === draw.twinCount) {
                    countClass += "bg-gray-800 text-white w-8 h-8 rounded-full shadow-md";
                } else {
                    countClass += "text-gray-900 bg-transparent text-sm w-8 h-8";
                }

                div.innerHTML = `
                    <span class="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 w-16 text-center">${draw.round}회</span>
                    <div class="flex gap-2 flex-1 justify-center items-center">
                        ${draw.numbers.map(num => {
                            const className = isTwin(num) ? 'ball-twin' : 'ball-normal';
                            return `<span class="w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full ${className} text-sm">${String(num).padStart(2, '0')}</span>`;
                        }).join('')}
                    </div>
                    <div class="w-20 flex justify-center">
                        <span onclick="event.stopPropagation(); toggleCountHighlight(${draw.twinCount})" 
                              class="${countClass}">
                            ${draw.twinCount}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                `;
                container.appendChild(div);
            });
        }
        
        // ... (차트 등 나머지 UI 로직 유지) ...
        function initChart() {
            const canvas = document.getElementById('twinChart');
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            const data = generateDistributionData(currentRange);
            
            twinChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels: data.labels,
                    datasets: [{
                        label: '당첨율',
                        data: data.values,
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.85)', 
                        borderColor: 'rgba(37, 99, 235, 1)', 
                        borderWidth: 2,
                        borderRadius: 0,
                        barThickness: 40,
                        datalabels: { display: false }
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    maintainAspectRatio: false,
                    plugins: {
                        legend: { display: false },
                        tooltip: { backgroundColor: 'rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.95)', padding: 12, titleFont: { size: 14, weight: 'bold' }, cornerRadius: 8 },
                    },
                    scales: {
                        y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { display: false }, title: { display: true, text: '횟수', align: 'end' } },
                        x: { grid: { display: false }, title: { display: true, text: '동형수 개수', align: 'end' } }
                    }
                },
                plugins: [minMaxLabelsPlugin]
            });
        }
        
        function generateDistributionData(range) {
            if (!allDrawData.length) return { labels: [], values: [] };
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, range);
            const counts = [0,0,0,0,0];
            data.forEach(d => {
                if (counts[d.twinCount] !== undefined) counts[d.twinCount]++;
            });
            return { 
                labels: ['0개', '1개', '2개', '3개', '4개'], 
                values: counts 
            };
        }
        
        function generateRoundData(range) {
            if (!allDrawData.length) return { labels: [], values: [] };
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, range);
            const labels = data.map(d => d.round).reverse();
            const values = data.map(d => d.twinCount).reverse();
            return { labels, values };
        }
        
        function changeChartMode(mode) {
            if (!twinChart) return;
            chartMode = mode;
            const distBtn = document.getElementById('chartTypeDistribution');
            const roundBtn = document.getElementById('chartTypeRound');
            
            if (mode === 'distribution') {
                distBtn.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors';
                roundBtn.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 rounded-lg transition-colors';
                
                const data = generateDistributionData(currentRange);
                twinChart.config.type = 'bar';
                twinChart.data.labels = data.labels;
                twinChart.data.datasets = [{ 
                    label: '횟수', 
                    data: data.values, 
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.85)', 
                    borderColor: 'rgba(37, 99, 235, 1)', 
                    borderWidth: 2, 
                    borderRadius: 0,
                    barThickness: 40
                }];
                twinChart.options.scales.x.ticks.display = true;
            } else {
                distBtn.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 rounded-lg transition-colors';
                roundBtn.className = 'px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors';
                
                twinChart.config.type = 'line';
                const data = generateRoundData(currentRange);
                const avgValue = data.values.length > 0 ? data.values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / data.values.length : 0;
                
                twinChart.data.labels = data.labels;
                twinChart.data.datasets = [
                    { label: '동형수 개수', data: data.values, backgroundColor: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.1)', borderColor: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 1)', borderWidth: 2, fill: true, tension: 0.4, pointRadius: 2 },
                    { label: `평균: ${avgValue.toFixed(1)}`, data: Array(data.values.length).fill(avgValue), borderColor: 'rgba(107, 114, 128, 0.6)', borderWidth: 1, borderDash: [8, 4], fill: false, pointRadius: 0 }
                ];
                twinChart.options.scales.x.ticks.display = currentRange < 50;
            }
            twinChart.update();
        }
        
        function updateSliderBackground(value) {
            const total = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const slider = document.getElementById('rangeSlider');
            if (slider) {
                const percent = ((value - 10) / (total - 10)) * 100;
                slider.style.setProperty('--value-percent', percent + '%');
            }
        }
        
        function updateRange(value) {
            currentRange = parseInt(value);
            document.getElementById('rangeDisplay').textContent = value + '회';
            updateSliderBackground(value);
            updateStatistics(currentRange);
            updatePresetButtons(currentRange);
            changeChartMode(chartMode);
            applyFilter();
            refreshAIAnalysis();
        }
        
        function updatePresetButtons(value) {
            const maxVal = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const buttons = { 50: 'preset50', 100: 'preset100', 500: 'preset500', 1203: 'preset1203' };
            Object.keys(buttons).forEach(key => {
                const btn = document.getElementById(buttons[key]);
                const targetValue = key === '1203' ? maxVal : parseInt(key);
                if (targetValue === value) btn.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium bg-blue-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors';
                else btn.className = 'px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 rounded-lg transition-colors';
            });
        }
        
        function setRange(value) {
            const maxVal = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const safeValue = value > maxVal ? maxVal : value;
            document.getElementById('rangeSlider').value = safeValue;
            updateRange(safeValue);
        }
        
        function openStatsModal() { 
            document.getElementById('statsModal').classList.remove('hidden'); 
            const statsSlider = document.getElementById('statsRangeSlider');
            if (statsSlider) {
                statsSlider.value = currentRange;
                updateStatsRange(currentRange);
            }
        }
        function closeStatsModal() { document.getElementById('statsModal').classList.add('hidden'); }
        
        function loadStatsData(statsRange) {
            if (!allDrawData.length) return;
            const tbody = document.getElementById('statsTableBody');
            tbody.innerHTML = '';
            
            const twinNums = [11, 22, 33, 44];
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, statsRange);
            
            const missedCounts = {};
            twinNums.forEach(n => { 
                missedCounts[n] = []; 
                let c = 0; 
                for(let i=data.length-1; i>=0; i--) { 
                    if(data[i].numbers.includes(n)) { 
                        c=0; 
                        missedCounts[n][i] = 0; 
                    } else { 
                        c++; 
                        missedCounts[n][i] = c; 
                    } 
                } 
            });
            
            const html = data.map((draw, idx) => {
                return `<tr>
                    <td class="sticky left-0 z-20 bg-white border-r border-b border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 text-center text-sm font-semibold text-black w-16">${draw.round}</td>
                    <td class="sticky left-16 z-10 bg-white border-r border-b border-gray-200 px-3 py-2 text-center text-sm font-bold w-20 text-black">${draw.twinCount}</td>
                    ${twinNums.map(n => {
                        const has = draw.numbers.includes(n);
                        return has 
                            ? `<td class="bg-gray-700 border-r border-b border-gray-200"></td>` 
                            : `<td class="bg-white border-r border-b border-gray-200 text-center text-[10px] text-gray-400">${missedCounts[n][idx]||''}</td>`;
                    }).join('')}
                </tr>`;
            }).join('');
            tbody.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function updateStatsRange(value) {
            const maxVal = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const display = value >= maxVal ? `전체(${maxVal})` : value;
            document.getElementById('statsRangeDisplay').textContent = display;
            const slider = document.getElementById('statsRangeSlider');
            if (slider) {
                const percent = ((value - 10) / (maxVal - 10)) * 100;
                slider.style.setProperty('--value-percent', percent + '%');
            }
            loadStatsData(parseInt(value));
        }
        
        function setStatsRange(value) {
            const maxVal = allDrawData.length || 1203;
            const safeValue = value > maxVal ? maxVal : value;
            document.getElementById('statsRangeSlider').value = safeValue;
            updateStatsRange(safeValue);
        }

        async function refreshAIAnalysis() {
            // 헤더 텍스트 업데이트 (Missing 패턴)
            if (allDrawData.length > 0) {
                const subjectRound = allDrawData[0].round;
                const targetRound = subjectRound + 1;
                const targetEl = document.getElementById('aiTargetInfo');
                if(targetEl) targetEl.textContent = `Target ${targetRound}회차_based on ${subjectRound}회차`;
            }

            const aiContent = document.getElementById('aiAnalysisContent');
            aiContent.innerHTML = `<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-4 text-gray-300"><div class="w-8 h-8 border-2 border-gray-300 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin"></div><span>AI가 데이터를 심층 분석 중입니다...</span></div>`;
            
            const data = allDrawData.slice(0, currentRange);
            const recentDraws = data.slice(0, 10).map(d => `${d.round}회:${d.twinCount}개`).join(', ');
            const allValues = data.map(d => d.twinCount);
            const avg = (allValues.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/allValues.length).toFixed(1);
            
            const subjectData = allDrawData[0];
            const subjectRound = subjectData.round;
            const targetRound = subjectRound + 1;

            await window.AIAnalysis.executeAnalysis({
                containerId: 'aiAnalysisContent',
                analysisType: '동형수 분석',
                subjectRound: subjectRound,
                targetRound: targetRound,
                customData: `
# [동형수(11, 22, 33, 44) 분석 데이터]
- 분석 범위: 최근 ${currentRange}회
- 평균 출현 개수: ${avg}개 (회차당)
- 최근 10회차 추이: ${recentDraws}
- 직전 회차(${subjectRound}회) 출현 개수: ${subjectData.twinCount}개
                `,
                customRules: `
# [분석 가이드]
1. 멸(0개) 지속 여부: 동형수가 최근 멸 구간인지, 필출 시점이 다가왔는지 분석.
2. 특정 번호 집중: 11, 22, 33, 44 중 유독 자주 나오는 번호나 오랫동안 안 나온 번호 식별.
3. 이월 패턴: 직전 회차에 나온 동형수가 다음 회차에 이월될 가능성 예측.
                `
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
